**CARRIER (rule 16). Committed in the repository, not in a workflow journal — F335's standing fix.**

*"Add a fourth lens whose only question is **'has the improvement removed what made this
dangerous?'** — critique #5 is that all three ⚠ items kept their label and lost their content."*

## §1 THE HEADLINE: THE SCREEN AND THE AUDIT ARE ESSENTIALLY UNCORRELATED

```
    n = 14 finalists, 60 verdicts, 4 lenses each
    Pearson  r   = +0.173
    Spearman rho = +0.222
    audit mean over all 60 verdicts = 1.85 / 4    min 1.0   max 3.0
```

⚠ **AND THE FOURTH LENS DID ITS JOB: `dangerIntact` is TRUE for only 4 of 14.** Ten finalists were
judged to have had the thing that made them dangerous sanded off — **which is the rev-76 critic's
finding 5 recurring inside rev 77's own round.** The lens exists to catch that and it caught it.

⚠ **CEILINGS, AND THEY BIND.** These are 14 of 75, selected BY the screen — so the correlation is
computed on a range-restricted set and would likely be weaker, not stronger, over all 75. Scores
are single-judge per lens, 0–4, no inter-rater check. **What survives regardless: the screen's own
#1 is the audit's #14, and no ordering claim from the screen alone should be trusted.**

## §2 THE THREE AMPLIFICATIONS THAT COMPLETED

**11 amplifications, the synthesis and the two late critics hit a session limit and did not run.**
What follows is what exists. **Every one of these three was told by the danger lens that its risk
had been sanded off, and each says what it put back.**

### DE LA MANO DE — the charola that names its cook

**REGISTER** food-and-hand-off; a painted image over a measured line drawing, the two meeting on a brass edge. Territory: beautiful-not-rigorous. **F331 LINE: ADULT** (paid, kept). The charolita is CUT from this concept — see the risk section; a child's object owes F18's locked scene and is not a shrunk adult tray.  
**ARTEFACT** A lithographed tin charola. 380 × 290 mm, rolled edge, six inks. **It holds plates, glasses and a bottle; it is not a plate and food does not touch it** — which is what a cantina charola has always been, and it is the claim the previous draft got wrong by calling itself a serving tray in front of a restaurateur.

The well is **deliberately unvarnished**; varnish only on the rim. The image is designed to abrade from the centre outward, in the shape of the plates set down on it.

Rim, in signwriter's brush script, one line: **DE LA MANO DE — [cook's name] — [month, year]**. Each run is named for whoever cooked during it. Small, at the foot: TACOMBI.

Reverse, one ink: the counter in orthographic section, drawn through `line_pass.py` — the 107.0 mm slab, the 19.9 mm brass nosing, the three sills at their three real heights. Not a caption. A second drawing.

**CUT from the previous draft:** the 240 × 180 charolita (it needs its own plates and its own press tool — the feasibility lens is right that "shares the plates" is false), and the word PROVECHO.

**One picture, split by a brass line 19.9 mm deep, and the split is the design.**

Below and around the counter's nosing — the slab, the aperture's cut edge, the bobble fringe, the menu card at the top of the frame — is **vector line and flat colour, drawn through the line pass off the 3D asset**. That is the owner's own locked style, and since rev 77 it is a thing this machine can actually make.

Above and through the hole — the cook's hands coming out with a plate, your own coming up to meet them, four tacos and a lime — is **painted**. Gouache, warm, unashamed.

The two registers meet exactly on the brass. That edge is the real boundary between what seventy-seven revisions know and what a person had to supply, and it is drawn, not printed in 4 pt. **The last draft said "the armature is painted over until nothing of it shows." That was the concept's worst sentence.** The measured half is now half the picture, in ink, visible at arm's length, and the honesty is structural.

**The counter is not level, and that is what the model is for.** `CNT_ZT` 1.2540 is authored un-dropped; `build.py` subtracts `rake_drop(x) = 0.047925 + 0.017750·x` per vertex, last. Above ground the counter top runs **1189.8 mm at its forward end to 1244.9 mm at its tail — a 55.1 mm climb over 3.106 m**, because the vehicle stands nose-down 1.02°. At the three serving bays it reads **1194.1 / 1205.2 / 1216.7 mm**. An illustrator who draws that counter level draws it wrong, and no tape measure and no single photograph will tell him otherwise.

**And the object carries no Volkswagen.** From the pass there is no roundel, no pressing, no T1 silhouette — only a hole Tacombi cut, a fringe Tacombi hung, a brass strip Tacombi nosed, and a menu card Tacombi painted. The completeness critic's top finding is that this whole programme is built on a third party's trade dress and nobody checked. **This is the pivot it asked for, already made.** It is plausibly the only saleable object on the bench that is clean, and that is worth more than the tray.

Then it wears. Then it is thrown away. Then you print the next one with the next cook's name on it.

**THE RISK IT STILL CARRIES** — **The danger lens scored the previous draft `dangerIntact = false` and it was right: every risk resolved to asking permission. Here is what I put back, named as put-back.**

**1. THE IMAGE IS DESIGNED TO BE DESTROYED BY ITS OWN USE. PUT BACK.** The draft promised a tray "the kind that outlives three refits" and specified clear varnish — the version where nothing happens. **Deleted.** The well is unvarnished and thin-inked; plates abrade the cook's hands away first, from the centre out. Live risk I am not solving: **some buyers will call that a manufacturing defect and demand a refund**, and a wholesaler may refuse it outright. The mitigation is not a caption — it is that the wear is photographed at 6 and 12 months and that record is the piece.

**2. IT NAMES AND PAYS A WORKER ON MERCHANDISE THE COMPANY SELLS. PUT BACK, AND THIS IS THE REAL ONE.** The draft said "I would paint him and get the consent" — a release form, i.e. a checkbox. **A named cook, paid a per-unit share, credited in the same brush script as the brand.** That is a **policy, not a picture**: once you name one cook you must decide about the next one, about the kitchen porter, and about every subsequent object. **And the tray outlives the employment — when he leaves you are selling a stranger's name.** I have no answer to that and am not pretending to. It is the price of being the only object in seventy-eight concepts that credits a human being.

**3. NO ARCHIVE PEOPLE. THE FABRICATION IS STRUCK.** The completeness critic established that "magenta puffer coat", "back to camera", "looking into the lens" and the headcounts of six and seven **exist in no file in this repository** — unpainted windows carrying consent arguments, rule 8 on an ethical decision. What the record actually holds is a man deleted by `MAN_ROWS, MAN_COLS = (420, 768), (90, 300)` in `probe_rev39_flank.py`, and a child's hair as residual contamination in F220. **Nobody from the archive appears on this object.** The hands are drawn from a new sitting, in the bus, with the named cook, paid. That is legally clean **and cheaper** — it is one afternoon that yields reference, release and the food correction at once.

**4. IT SAYS ON A SOLD OBJECT THAT THE MEASUREMENT CANNOT DRAW THE THING BEING SOLD.** The two-register split makes visible, at 380 mm, that seventy-seven revisions of geometry stop at a brass edge and a person has to draw the taco. **That is a critique of the money spent, printed and sold by the man who spent it.** The draft softened this into a service claim ("the one thing an illustrator cannot guess"). Unsoftened.

**5. IT PUTS THE VIEWER'S OWN HANDS IN FRAME.** Kept. It tells you you are the one being served. Some will find that presumptuous; that is the transaction.

**6. WHAT IT STILL CANNOT DO — DECLARED, per completeness finding 8.** No roundel is visible from the pass, so **this object cannot advance the emblem and F191's hold is untouched by it.** It does not compose the mark out at an unjudgeable 8 mm, which is worse; it is simply not in the room. **The live emblem lead is elsewhere and must not be lost with this concept: F333 records that the VW badge TRACES CLEANLY through the line pass — "not wrong in line" — with its own ceiling (hubcap, square-on, one scale; the nose roundel at 18° has never been traced).**

**7. F94, NOT SPEC §10.10.** The artwork bar's register row is **F94**, which says the bar *"is in no live carrier AS A BAR"* and that **four of its seven named parts have no row at all — including the menu strips and cards.** The menu card is in this frame. So this object does not escape the bar on a technicality; it **reproduces** that card from the photograph rather than stylising it, and says so.

**8. F92, checked by object (rule 34):** F92's object is the Playa hero as a delivery frame with `playa_env.py`. This is a counter. No terrace, no palms, no second delivery frame. **Not acted on.** Its sentence — *"the emotional bar that sits ABOVE clinical accuracy"* — is what this concept is for, quoted rather than borrowed.

**9. SANITATION IS A ONE-QUESTION CHECK, NOT A DESIGN CHANGE.** Litho tinplate holding crockery is what cantina charolas have done for sixty years, but **if his inspector says no, it is a retail object and not a room object.** Ask on day one.

**WHAT THE MODEL CONTRIBUTES** — **Not a wireframe. Half the drawing, plus one fact no photograph in the archive resolves.**

**1. THE RAKE — the model's one irreplaceable contribution here.** The counter climbs 55.1 mm from nose to tail because the whole vehicle is sheared 17.75 mm/m. That is invisible to a tape measure held against one spot and it is exactly what puts the hands at the right height in a hand-off drawing. The previous draft published "counter top 1254 mm above ground" — the **authored un-dropped constant**, 49–64 mm high depending on station, produced by the retired flat 65 mm scalar. `t1_detail.py` warns about this error in its own comment twenty lines above the constant (*"the 'Subtract 0.065 for above-ground' line that used to be here is WRONG… the drop is a LINE, not a scalar"*) and the draft made it anyway. Retracted, rule 13.

**2. THE LINE PASS EXISTS AND THIS IS A LEGITIMATE CONSUMER.** `line_pass.py` shipped at rev 77 (F332). The measured half of this image is a real output of it, not a promise. **Route: `OBJECT` over a duplicated-and-joined copy — `COLLECTION` is a silent no-op that draws the whole vehicle (F332).**

**3. THE PASS CAMERA DOES NOT EXIST AND IS THREE LINES.** `studio.py` carries thirteen cameras. The nearest, `"counter"`, sits at loc (3.40, 5.20, 1.98) — **5.20 m outboard** of a counter whose outer face is `CNT_Y_OUT` 1.1660. Nothing in this tree stands at the pass. Adding one is a dict entry.

**4. WHAT THE MODEL CANNOT GIVE, STATED AT FULL STRENGTH.** No human geometry, no food geometry — the plancha is a bare `rrect(0.560, 0.780)` prism that has never had anything on it. **No AO or normal pass exists**, so the *second* half of the owner's locked clause — "shading and occlusion sampled from the 3D asset" — is unavailable and this image does not claim it. **Y is chosen everywhere in this model and never measured**, so the galley recession behind the aperture plane is a construction. The model is trustworthy in the aperture plane and in the rake; behind it, a person is drawing.

**5. RETRACTED FROM THE PREVIOUS DRAFT (rule 13):** *"All nine reference photographs are shot from further back… the model is the only way to stand where the customer stands."* **Both halves fail.** The record names **seven** distinct target-bus frames, not nine (plus two green-vehicle frames and a school bus), and five `IMG_*` files are byte-duplicates of them — a duplicate is not corroboration. And **`ref_nolita_flank.jpg` is a first-person customer POV with the viewer's own hand and forearm in frame** holding a Monopolio bottle, bus behind. I opened it. The true, smaller, still-useful claim: **no frame is taken at a serving hatch looking in, so the geometry of a 515.5 × 403.0 aperture from a standing adult's eye is not in the reference set.** The flank frame is now cited as *grounding* — it is proof that hand-in-frame is how customers actually photograph this bus.

**MEASURED FIGURES** — **Every figure below was watched print this session against live source at HEAD (rev 77, `0323ebe`).**

**COUNTER — station-corrected, the previous draft's headline error.** `t1_detail.py`: `CNT_ZT, CNT_ZB = 1.2540, 1.1470  # 107 mm thick`; `CNT_Y_IN, CNT_Y_OUT = 0.8450, 1.1660  # 321 mm plan depth`; `CNT_X0 = 0.9180`; `CNT_X1 = T.X_TAIL − CNT_OVERHANG = −1.873 − 0.315 = −2.1880` (the source's own `# −2.173` comment is stale). `t1_core.py`: `RAKE_Z0 = 0.047925`, `RAKE_DZDX = 0.017750`.

    counter top above ground   1189.8 mm at x +0.918  ->  1244.9 mm at x −2.188
                               a 55.1 mm CLIMB over 3.106 m
    fascia bottom AG           1082.8 mm at x +0.918
    at the three bay centres BAY_CX_SPEC (+0.6720, +0.0470, −0.5980):
      counter top AG   1194.1 / 1205.2 / 1216.7 mm
      sill      AG     1312.1 / 1323.2 / 1334.7 mm
      head      AG     1715.1 / 1726.2 / 1737.7 mm
      aperture mid     1513.6 mm at bay 0

**RETRACTED (rule 13):** "counter top 1254 mm above ground", "sill 1307 / head 1710 above ground". 1254 is the authored **un-dropped** constant; 1307/1710 use `RIDE_DROP` 0.0650, which is the drop at `X_DROP_REF` +0.96197 **only** and which `build.py` retired as a frame conversion at rev 8. Every above-ground height on this object now carries its station.

**THE OPEN NUMBER, AND IT IS THE ONE THE ILLUSTRATOR NEEDS.** `REF_MEASUREMENTS` §6 reads the brass nosing off `ref_side.jpg` at slope **−0.0385 px/px over x_img 360…930** = 21.9 px. On that region's own vertical ruler (the slab, 22.65 px = 107 ± 5 mm → 4.724 mm/px) that is **103.7 mm of rise**. The model over the same image span says **45.9 mm**. **SIGN AGREES — lower at the nose, higher at the tail; the photograph sees the rake.** **MAGNITUDE IS 2.26× APART AND UNRESOLVED.** Ceiling: a 3/4 view, one frame, and the image slope also carries perspective convergence, though `REF_MEASUREMENTS` line 68 records the long body lines as *"parallel, not convergent"*, which weakens that excuse. **It is cheaply decidable: `probe_rev39_flank.py` carries a solved warp into `ref_side.jpg`'s pixel frame — project the model's nosing through it and read the image slope.** Not run. Do not draw a magnitude until it is.

**VERIFIED UNCHANGED FROM THE PREVIOUS DRAFT.** `CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860` with source comment `# 19.9 mm of gold in elevation`; `FRINGE_R, FRINGE_PITCH, FRINGE_INSET = 0.0090, 0.0260, 0.0130`; `GAL_SEAM_X = −0.6140  # measured back-wall material change`; galley strip light `emit=(1.000, 0.918, 0.790)`; brass linear `(0.6600, 0.4750, 0.1750)`; aperture 515.5 × 403.0, r 55, **three, equal**; `gal_menucard` 96 × 311 mm.

**THE FRINGE COUNT IS NOT PUBLISHED HERE.** `bobble_fringe()` derives it by `poly_offset` then `_resample_closed(out, FRINGE_PITCH)` — it is a derived quantity nobody has printed. The pool quotes 64 and 65. **Print it before drawing it.**

**NOT USED, DELIBERATELY:** the 109.5 / 129.5 pillar asymmetry (retracted rev 76) and its "0.67 σ" replacement — **F336 re-derives 0.54 σ**, since asymmetry = 2c₁ − c₀ − c₂ makes the pitches non-independent. Nothing here leans on either.

**FIRST SHIPPABLE STEP** — **IN HIS HANDS THIS WEEK. Half a day, £0, no illustrator, no tin, no render queue.**

**1. Add the `pass` camera to `studio.py`** — three lines beside the existing thirteen:
`"pass": dict(loc=(0.672, 1.520, 1.600), tgt=(0.672, 0.845, 1.514), persp=True)` — standing at arm's reach outboard of `CNT_Y_OUT` 1.1660, at bay 0's measured centre `+0.6720`, aimed at that bay's aperture mid-height **1513.6 mm AG**. **Eye height 1.600 m is a DECLARED POSE** — no human geometry exists anywhere in this tree — and it is labelled one.

**2. Run `line_pass.py` through it** over the counter, aperture edge, fringe, brass nosing and menu card — **`OBJECT` route over a duplicated-and-joined copy**, because `COLLECTION` is a silent no-op (F332). Redraw the fringe and the nosing as true geometry per F333 rather than accepting the fragments.

**3. Print it A3 — and print only the half the machine knows.** The counter, the cut edge, the fringe, the brass, the menu card: finished line, flat colour. **Everything through the hole is blank paper.**

**4. Hand it to him with a pencil and nothing else.** Not a nice painting to smile at — that step assumed the expensive resource and tested nothing. This sheet asks its question by being half-empty: here is exactly what seventy-seven revisions can draw, and here is the hole where a person has to.

**5. Ask, with the question tool, multiple choice, the reference crop attached** — two questions only he can answer, and neither is about whether he likes it:
 **(a) What crosses the counter?** paper-lined basket / plate / paper sheet on the slab / hand to hand, no vessel — and how many.
 **(b) Whose name goes on the rim?** the cook who is there now, named and paid a per-unit share / the kitchen collectively / no name, TACOMBI only / not yet — ask him first.
 **Premise checked before asking** (rule 52, F229): this is not the papel-picado question he closed at rev 76, and it is not a factory-part dimension available in catalogue literature. It is his business and his employee, and only he can rule it.

**6. In the same conversation, the two gates:** does the shop already sell serveware, and will his inspector accept litho tinplate in the room.

**AND ONE CHEAP MEASUREMENT WORTH MORE THAN THE SHEET.** Project the model's brass nosing through **`probe_rev39_flank.py`'s existing solved warp** into `ref_side.jpg`'s pixel frame and read its image slope against `REF_MEASUREMENTS` §6's **−0.0385 px/px**. That closes or opens the **2.26×** disagreement about how much the counter climbs — the one number the illustrator actually needs, and the only one on this object that is still open. An hour, and it is a real result either way.

**HOW IT ANSWERED THE FOUR LENSES** — **FEASIBILITY (2/4) — attacks 1, 3, 4, 5, 6 conceded and removed from the critical path; 2, 7, 8, 9 answered.**
(1,3,5,6) **Conceded entire.** The deliverable is reclassified: **the artefact is the IMAGE.** No tin, no plates, no press tooling, no MOQ, no charolita is quoted until he has seen it. Every manufacturing error the lens found is now off the critical path rather than corrected. (4) **Conceded — the charolita is cut.** (2, the real one — *no illustrator exists*) **Answered structurally: the painting job is halved.** Everything below the brass is the line pass, which shipped at rev 77 and which this machine provably runs. A person paints hands, a plate and food over a finished measured drawing — not a whole tray. (7, *model contribution is half a day*) **Refuted with a figure the lens did not have: the rake.** 55.1 mm of climb across the counter, invisible to any tape measure, plus half the ink. The lens was right that a `pass` camera is three lines; it was wrong that three lines is all the model gives. (8, *no gate can catch "generic"*) **Conceded and a gate built** — the prior-art bench, below. (9) Answered by taking the *named and paid* branch, which is cheaper than the archive branch, not dearer.

**ORIGINALITY (1/4) — the hardest lens, and it lands.**
(1, *Coca-Cola red-rim / Mexican charola*) **Conceded: the format is 129 years old.** Answered by breaking it where prior art is silent — **an unvarnished well engineered to abrade under service**, and **a face split between machine line and human paint on a measured 19.9 mm boundary.** No charola does either. (2, *PROVECHO has an Etsy market page*) **Conceded. The word is killed**, replaced by an attribution formula completed with a cook's name — a string no competitor can copy without naming their own cook. (3, *`ref_nolita_flank.jpg` already holds the customer-POV hand*) **Verified — I opened it, and the critic is right.** Retracted in `whyThisModel`, and the frame is now cited as grounding. (5, *invisible contribution is not a contribution*) **The strongest attack in all four audits, and it forced the concept's shape.** The model is now half the printed picture. (6, *the premise is an assigned slot*) **Conceded outright** — finding 4 wrote it, down to "the tray crossing the brass line." (7) Both figures retracted. **I did not take fix C (die-cut to the bus's side elevation): it would import the exact VW trade-dress exposure that is this object's cleanest property.** I took A, B (as a second drawing, not a confession), E, F, G.

**OWNER AND BRAND (2/4) — the fatal finding is fixed.**
(A1, *carries neither wordmark nor bus*) **Fixed without stepping back and losing the hand-off: the menu card sits at the top of the frame**, reproduced from photograph under F94, plus TACOMBI on the rim and a named cook. The object is now unmistakably his. (A2, *tinplate is not service ware*) **Conceded and the claim changed** — it holds plates, food does not touch it, and the inspector is asked on day one. (A3, *charolita breaks F18's scene lock*) **Conceded; cut.** F331 requires a children's line of equal standing, not a shrunk adult tray. (A4) Fixed — the first step is a build. (A5) Release before plates, and now before painting. (A6) The food is asked, not chosen.

**DANGER (1/4, dangerIntact FALSE) — the lens was right and its fixes are taken almost entire.** Fixes 1 (the well dies under the plates), 3 (pay and name him), 4 (the measured palette replaces the mood direction), 5 (the reverse is a drawing, not an apology) and the revised ask are all in. Fix 2 (burn scars, a phone) I take in part: **the phone stays, the scars are not mine to specify** — that is a decision for the man being painted, asked at the sitting. Fix 6 (child's eyeline) I **refuse here and hand on**: it is a genuinely better children's object than a shrunk tray, and F331 plus F18's locked scene mean it deserves its own concept rather than smuggling in as a size variant.

**CROSS-CUTTING, from the two completeness critics.** Tree re-based on **HEAD = rev 77**, not rev 76. **F331 line declared (adult).** The AO pass is declared absent rather than assumed. The aperture rectangle is **not** re-substrated — this object is not the 515.5 × 403.0 in another material. **No printed confession on the face**: the danger critic's *"CONFESS thirty times out of seventy-five"* charge is answered by making the measurement change the object (the split, the rake, the wear) instead of narrating it in 4 pt.

### CIENTO DIECIOCHO Y UNA (118 + 1)

**REGISTER** Children's line under F331 — free, handed across the counter, no price and no figures on the object. Time-based, but the direction is reversed from the concept I was given: the artefact does not switch on. It goes OUT, over about ten minutes, in a measured order, and one light stays.  
**ARTEFACT** A die-cut sticker, 200 mm long (1:20.325), printed on off-the-shelf long-persistence strontium-aluminate glow vinyl. THE PHOSPHOR IS THE SUBSTRATE, NOT A PRINT PASS. Digitally printed (eco-solvent or UV) in three inks — RED #C43024, CREAM #CED0C8, and a warm dark for line and script — then contour-cut on a plotter reading a registration mark. No die, no screens, no tooling, minimum order 10.

Everything that glows is a KNOCK-OUT to bare substrate, and the knock-outs are printed at TWO densities:
• CLEAN knock-out, 100 % substrate: the galley strip alone — one 3.4 mm slot behind the counter line.
• VEILED knock-out, ~55 % cream halftone over substrate: the three shut bay panels (25.36 × 19.83 mm, r 2.71) and all 118 drip-rail lamps, each a 0.60 mm dot on the measured 1.403 mm pitch.

DAYLIGHT: a shut red-and-cream panel van. Three closed panels where the serving holes are. A row of dull dots along the drip rail. SEÑOR TACOMBI in bright silver on the flank (F157). The flank paisley as one flat. Plain light discs on the hubcaps — no glyph. Nothing projects past the sheet metal: no counter, no tail board, no propped hatch, no festoon strung out. It is not a taqueria. It is the vehicle a taqueria was cut into.

LIGHTS OUT: the whole bus is lit — 118 lamps, three panels, one galley slot. Then it starts closing. The veiled knock-outs began dimmer and cross the eye's threshold first: the lamps go out, then the panels. The galley slot is clean substrate and stays lit alone, for hours.

The last light in the bus is the one someone is working under. Only the sun turns it back on.

BACKING CARD (children's line, no figures, no tolerances): the name; "118 lamps and one light"; "charge it in the sun"; and one sentence — the lamps go out because the party is over, and the last light is on because someone is still in there.

The concept I was handed was a bus going to sleep. Chronicle Books shipped that in 2016 — GOODNIGHT, GOODNIGHT, CONSTRUCTION SITE: GLOW IN THE DARK EDITION, work vehicles bedding down under a uniform phosphorescent overprint, for the same five-year-old. And BUENAS NOCHES, COMBI is Buenas noches, Luna with the noun swapped. Both collisions are conceded and the title is dead.

What survives is a fact nobody in four audits found, and I watched it print. Across the four geometry modules, `grep -c "emit=("` returns **0 / 0 / 1 / 0**. In a 229-object, 366,490-face vehicle carrying 118 festoon lamps, two headlamps, two indicators and a lit interior, exactly ONE material emits light: the galley work strip, `emit=(1.000, 0.918, 0.790)` at `GAL_LUM 3.04`. The 118 lamps have no emission at all — `t1_detail.py`'s own comment says "Rendered as unlit pearl glass: this project has no emissive material," and `probe_rev71_bulbs.py` records that they render unlit pearl white while both in-service photographs show them lit and warm.

So the model has believed this object's proposition for 77 revisions: the party lights are dark, and the work light is on. I did not apply that metaphor to the build. It IS the build.

The sticker makes it physical. One phosphor, two print densities. A dimmer deposit of the same material crosses the eye's threshold earlier, so extinction has an ORDER without a second pass, a second screen, or a second cent. The child turns off the light: 118 lamps, three panels, one slot, all lit. Ten minutes later the festoon is gone, the serving holes are gone, and a single warm-ish slot is still burning behind the counter, and it burns for hours in a dark bedroom.

This answers F18's locked scene by physics rather than by apology. The lock reads "nothing but the bus, die-cut tight, plus the sun and the papel picado." A phosphorescent sticker does not delete the sun — THE SUN IS THE MECHANISM. It is the only thing that charges the bus. This is the one artefact on the bench that satisfies that clause by working, and the previous draft apologised for not having a sun it literally runs on.

And the daylight state is the danger the last draft called "asleep." A shut combi with nothing projecting past the sheet metal is not a sleeping taqueria. It is a panel van — the vehicle before the conversion, or after it. That silhouette is what a child holds all day, and the glow is what happens when the room goes dark and it closes anyway.

The word "asleep" is banned from the object and from the card.

**THE RISK IT STILL CARRIES** — THE DANGER LENS SCORED THIS FALSE. IT WAS RIGHT, AND HERE IS WHAT I PUT BACK — every item is in the object or the card, not in a tone.

1. DECAY IS NOW THE MECHANIC, NOT A MATERIAL FOOTNOTE. The old draft promised "all 118 bulbs come on" and never mentioned that they go out. The bus does not switch on. It CLOSES, while the child watches, and only the sun can reopen it. A nightlight consoles; this does not.
2. "ASLEEP" IS BANNED. The state is CERRADO. The word appeared five times in the draft I was given and is the exact sanding the lens identified.
3. THE CHILD'S-EYE SENTENCE IS ON THE CARD, UNSOFTENED: the lamps go out because the party is over; the last light is on because someone is still in there. In a taqueria family the truck is the reason a parent is not home at bedtime. A glow sticker goes on a bedroom ceiling. This puts that light in a dark room for hours.
4. THE DAYLIGHT SILHOUETTE IS THE PANEL VAN. Nothing projects past the sheet metal, the three holes are shut: it is the vehicle before the conversion, or after it. Stated as the subject.
5. THE F18 LOCK IS ARGUED, NOT CONCEDED. The old draft pre-surrendered. THE SUN IS THE MECHANISM.
6. AND ONE THE LENS DID NOT NAME, WHICH I ADD: kept in a drawer, this object is dead. That is a real condition on ownership and it is printed, not hidden.

WHAT COULD STILL FAIL OR OFFEND, HONESTLY:
(a) THE PARENT WHO IS NOT HOME. Handing a five-year-old an object whose subject is a parent still at work can land as tender or as cruel, and I cannot measure which. That is the owner's call and it is the FIRST question, not a designer's judgement. If it is wrong it is wrong on a child.
(b) THE GREEN IS UNRESOLVED. Strontium aluminate glows ~520 nm; the single emissive material in the build is #FFF6E6. The last light will be the WRONG COLOUR. Two admissible routes — an amber overprint on the galley slot only, at a real brightness cost, or accept it and say so — and the previous draft ASSERTED no ink fixes it without watching that print. It is now an experiment, not an assertion.
(c) THE CURVES ARE NOT MEASURED. Everything about "ten minutes" and the two densities crossing threshold at different times is the material's known behaviour. If the veiled and clean knock-outs extinguish together, THE CONCEPT IS DEAD and the first step kills it this week.
(d) THE SILHOUETTE IS STILL A T1. Plain discs and an edge-on roundel remove the marks; they do not remove the trade dress. Giveaway is a different exposure from a sold SKU, not zero exposure.
(e) IT IS STILL A SCENE DEPARTURE UNTIL HE RULES. A companion to F18, not F18.
(f) THE ORIGINAL BET SURVIVES UNCHANGED: a five-year-old may simply prefer the open, working, colourful bus.

**WHAT THE MODEL CONTRIBUTES** — THE ONE-EMITTER FACT, WHICH IS THE CONCEPT. `grep -c "emit=("` over `t1_core.py` / `t1_shell.py` / `t1_detail.py` / `t1_mats.py` returns 0 / 0 / 1 / 0 — a single emissive material in the whole vehicle, the galley strip at `emit=(1.000, 0.918, 0.790)`, `GAL_LUM = 3.04`, encoding to #FFF6E6. No photograph shows this. No render has ever managed it. It is a property only of the asset, discoverable only by grepping the asset, and it is the artefact's entire narrative.

THE LAMP STATIONS. 118 balls on 117 intervals at a realised pitch of 28.5197 mm, from `BULB_X0 = T._aft(-1.8000) = -1.63681` m (`O_OLD = X_AXLE_R - X_TAIL_OLD = 1.008`, `O_NEW = 0.773`), `BULB_X1 = 1.7000`, `BULB_PITCH = 0.0286`. Nothing but the model can place them, and the figure in general circulation — 115 — is stale prose from `t1_detail.py`'s own comment.

THE SHUT STATE IS NEW VEHICLE GEOMETRY, AND IT IS THE SHIP. It does not exist: `STATE.md` reads "open serving apertures on +Y: 3", `OPEN_BAYS = (0, 1, 2)`, `SHOW_SIDE = 1`, and `glass_bays()`'s own docstring says "the three show-side serving bays get none." Three flat closures at the `BAY_CX` stations, 515.5 × 403.0 r 55 (`BAY_W = 0.5155`, equal, measured), behind `T1_BAY_SHUT`. Plus the nine parts `STATE.md` names as projecting — counter, counter_nosing, counter_top, tail_board, tail_board_stay, tb_bulbflex, tb_bulbs, tb_edge_dark, tb_edge_red — excluded, taking length 4.317 → 4.065 m. This is the only concept on the bench whose print spec REQUIRES new geometry, and it is about twenty lines.

THE CUT PATH FROM ALPHA, NOT FROM LINE ART. `T1_ALPHA=1` is built, guarded and has never been run in 77 revisions; `deliver.py` refuses without it. Running it is a finding.

THE SIDE ORTHOGRAPHIC CAMERA at `loc=(0.0, 26.0, 1.52)` fits, and looks at the +Y show flank where `bulb_string(side=1)` hangs. The clipped front and rear cameras are irrelevant to this object.

**MEASURED FIGURES** — WATCHED PRINT THIS SESSION, OFF LIVE SOURCE AT HEAD f06eb09 (rev 77):

EMISSION — the spine. `grep -c "emit=("`: t1_core 0, t1_shell 0, t1_detail 1, t1_mats 0. The single hit is `emit=(1.000, 0.918, 0.790), estr=GAL_LUM`, `GAL_LUM = 3.04`. Encodes to sRGB(255, 246, 230) = #FFF6E6.

LAMPS. `BULB_X0 = _aft(-1.8000) = -1.63681` m; `BULB_X1 = 1.7000`; span 3.33681 m; `BULB_PITCH = 0.0286` → 117 intervals, `range(n+1)` → **118 balls**, realised pitch **28.5197 mm**. `BULB_R = 0.0110` (22.0 mm lamp) → **6.520 mm of air** at built size. The brief's "115" is stale prose from `t1_detail.py`'s own comment; the concepts are right.
⚠ PITCH PROVENANCE DISAGREES WITH ITSELF IN LIVE SOURCE: `t1_detail.py` says "a MEASURED 28.8 ± 2.0 mm"; `t1_shell.py` says "measured BULB_PITCH (28.6 ± 1.0". Carried, not resolved.
⚠ `BULB_R` IS NOT MEASURED. `t1_detail.py`: "Pitch, bulb size and the fact that it is show side only are INFERRED." So the drawn dot size is a drawing choice, and shrinking it violates nothing.

SCALE, at 200 mm = 1:20.325. Pitch **1.403 mm**. Built lamp 1.082 mm → gap **0.321 mm**. Drawn dot 0.60 mm → gap **0.803 mm**. At 90 mm (1:45.167): pitch 0.631 mm, gap 0.144 mm. Aperture **25.36 × 19.83 mm r 2.71**. Hubcap disc **4.28 mm**.

APERTURES. `BAY_W = 0.5155` ("equal, measured"), 403.0 tall, r 55, `OPEN_BAYS = (0, 1, 2)`, `SHOW_SIDE = 1` (+Y). `STATE.md`: "open serving apertures on +Y: 3", "bay widths 0.516 0.515 0.516".

PALETTE — AND A ONE-LEVEL ERROR I CAUGHT. The authored albedo `RED = (0.5520, 0.0294, 0.0176)` encodes to sRGB(196, **48**, 36) = **#C43024**. The concept I was handed printed #C43124, and so does `t1_mats.py`'s OWN COMMENT ("= sRGB(196,49,36)"). Green is one level off in both. Rule 10 exactly — a claim in a source comment is not a measurement. Corrected. `CREAM = (0.6172, 0.6308, 0.5776)` → sRGB(206, 208, 200) = **#CED0C8**, exact.

PROJECTING PARTS, 9, `STATE.md` by name: counter, counter_nosing, counter_top, tail_board, tail_board_stay, tb_bulbflex, tb_bulbs, tb_edge_dark, tb_edge_red. Length 4.317 m with, 4.065 m without.

OFF SIDE. `SPEC.md` line 138: "Side, off side (−Y) | twin outward-hinged cargo doors + three glazed windows in the same bay positions | **E** (never photographed)."

MACHINE. `grep -c "closed" line_pass.py` = **0**. `sheet.py`: `fill(x, y, w, h)` axis-aligned only; `poly(close=True)` strokes, never fills. `line_pass.py` lines 119–129: `source_type="COLLECTION"` is a documented silent no-op. `deliver.py` line 154 refuses without `T1_ALPHA=1`.

CEILING, STATED: the afterglow curves are NOT MEASURED. Every claim about "ten minutes" is the material's known behaviour, not a figure I watched print. That measurement is the first shippable step, and no number goes on any object or card until it prints.

**FIRST SHIPPABLE STEP** — THIS WEEK, ZERO DOLLARS, AND IT TESTS THE BET RATHER THAN THE DECORATION — because the bet is the extinction ORDER, and if that fails the concept is dead and should die on a scrap of vinyl, not in production.

MONDAY AFTERNOON, THE EXPERIMENT (this is the measurement, and no figure goes on any object until it prints):
Take one A4 sheet of off-the-shelf glow vinyl. Cut a 40 mm strip. Leave one third bare, veil one third with a ~55 % grey halftone or marker, and swipe amber over the last third. Charge all three under a desk lamp for 60 s. Photograph in the dark at FIXED exposure and fixed distance at t = 0, 1, 5, 15 and 60 min. That gives three afterglow curves, the differential-extinction claim tested directly, and the green/amber trade priced — the three things the previous draft asserted and never watched.

SAME AFTERNOON, THE OBJECT:
Print the DAYLIGHT state at 200 mm on paper: shut bus, RED #C43024 (corrected — the source comment and the incoming concept were both one level off on green), CREAM #CED0C8, warm dark line, SEÑOR TACOMBI in bright silver, three shut bay panels, 118 lamp dots at 0.60 mm on 1.403 mm pitch, plain hubcap discs. Cut it out with a scalpel. This is a MOCK — the shut geometry is not built yet and I will say so rather than pass a hand-drawing off as a render.

IN HIS HANDS: the paper bus on his fridge, the vinyl strip beside it, and ONE photograph pair — t = 1 min and t = 15 min — showing the veiled third already dark while the clean third is still lit. He holds the entire proposition in one hand: the shut van by day, and the proof that it closes in an order.

AND ASK HIM THE TWO QUESTIONS THAT DECIDE IT, WITH THE QUESTION TOOL, AS MULTIPLE CHOICE, WITH THAT PHOTOGRAPH ATTACHED (a revision has been lost to sending figures and forgetting to ask):
1. THE SENTENCE — "the last light is on because someone is still in there," on a child's card. Tender, or too much? This is (a) in the risk list and it is not mine to decide.
2. THE SCENE — F18's lock reads "plus the sun." Here the sun is the only thing that charges the object. Does that satisfy the clause, or is this a companion to F18 rather than F18?
Do NOT re-ask the papel picado; F330 closed it.

WHAT I WILL NOT DO: put the daylight print next to the Chronicle glow edition and an Etsy campervan decal and ask him whether ours is a different object. That test was suggested and it is the wrong one — the daylight state IS the same object as theirs, and admitting it proves nothing. The difference is in the ten minutes, so the ten minutes is what gets photographed.

**HOW IT ANSWERED THE FOUR LENSES** — FEASIBILITY (2/4) — seven attacks, all conceded or answered, none dodged.
(1) SCALE SET BY THE WRONG NUMBER. Conceded outright: gap governs bridging, not pitch, and at 200 mm the built gap is 0.321 mm, which dot gain closes. Fixed at the LAMP, not the sheet — `BULB_R` is INFERRED by the source's own words, so a 0.60 mm dot on the measured 1.403 mm pitch gives 0.803 mm of air and violates no measured figure. And the process change deletes the failure mode rather than mitigating it: the lamps are KNOCK-OUTS in ink over a glow substrate, so there is no phosphor deposit to bridge at all.
(2) "A CLOSED CUT PATH IS WHAT LINE ART EMITS" IS FALSE. Verified — `grep -c closed line_pass.py` = 0, and F333 measured 182 strokes with a 156-point longest run. The MODEL paragraph is rewritten: the line pass is the DRAWING, the ALPHA is the CUT. `T1_ALPHA=1` → `deliver.py`'s trim (F161) → marching squares → one closed loop.
(3) THE SHUT BUS DOES NOT EXIST. Conceded, and promoted from an unstated liability to THE SHIP. `T1_BAY_SHUT`, three closures, nine named parts excluded, selection by duplicate-and-join with `source_type="OBJECT"` — never COLLECTION, per F332 — with the verify row WATCHED FAILING on the open build first (rules 3, 13).
(4) NO POLYGON FILL. Verified. Answered: flat colour comes from one emission-only Blender render at 600 dpi (200 mm = 4724 px), line laid over. The three bay panels are axis-aligned rounded rects in side elevation, so `sheet.py` can already lay those; only the body needs the render. If screens ever return, `poly_fill` goes in in the same edit as its first use.
(5) COST UNDERSTATED 3–5×, WRONG PROCESS. Conceded. Screens and die abandoned for digital-on-glow-substrate, which kills tooling, minimums and every registration error at once.
(6) COST/AUDIENCE COLLISION, WHICH THE OLD RISK LIST MISSED. Answered by (5) plus an F331 declaration: children's line, free, handed over, no price on the object.
(7) CHARGING IS A PLACEMENT CONSTRAINT. Accepted and promoted to a card line — "charge it in the sun" — and it is now the F18 argument rather than a footnote.

ORIGINALITY (1/4) — the hardest lens, and it wins most of the argument.
Collisions 1–5 CONCEDED WHOLESALE: the Chronicle glow edition, the Goodnight Moon title formula, glow vinyl's own stock demo, the VW-decal-plus-glow market, and Tacombi's default SKU. THE TITLE IS DEAD. What is not conceded is that the object survives them, because all five are UNIFORM reveals — one image switching on. This object's mechanic is DIFFERENTIAL EXTINCTION: a measured ORDER of going out, which a uniform overprint physically cannot produce and which the material's stock demo does not do. I took their fix (D)(i) and improved it — no second phosphor, one substrate at two print densities, so it costs nothing.
(6) REGISTER DRIFT: fixed. Every figure comes OFF the card.
(7) THIRD PASS AT ONE DRAWING: refuted — it is the SHUT bus, which is new geometry.
(8) "SCALE BY PHOSPHOR ANSWERS HOW BIG, NOT WHAT": conceded, demoted to a production note.
(9) "THE RENDER HAS NEVER MANAGED EITHER IS BURIED": taken, promoted to the first paragraph, and sharpened with the one-emitter grep they did not have.
(10) STATE.md's caveat weaker than F333 supports: accepted and now moot — the glyph is gone.
THEIR FIX (A), GLOW THE OFF SIDE — REFUSED ON THE MEASUREMENT, NOT DODGED. `SPEC.md` line 138 grades it E, never photographed; its two colliding features are BOTH grade E and contradict each other; shown its sightlines the owner answered "cannot tell from this crop"; and the flank guard there is a LABELLED regression catcher at 804.9 mm meaning "it has not moved," NOT "it is right." Printing the project's largest guess in the one medium where it is the ONLY visible thing, on a child's dark wall, is rule 8's defect at scale. Kept as a named adult-line companion, where a confession belongs.

OWNER AND BRAND (1.5/4) — all eight attacks bind and all eight are actioned.
A1 TRADEMARK, four parts: hubcap glyphs replaced by plain discs; the nose roundel absent by PROJECTION (a side elevation sees it edge-on — verifiable, not a taste choice); the T1 silhouette is unavoidable and goes to a one-paragraph IP opinion BEFORE any sold run, with the giveaway/sold distinction declared on the concept; and Tacombi's own marks go on.
A2 BRAND ABSENT: SEÑOR TACOMBI in F157's owner-ruled bright silver, plus the flank paisley as one flat.
A3 SPEC §10.10: the rule-34 paragraph is written and it cites **F94** — "the artwork bar is in no live carrier AS A BAR," four of seven named parts have no row — not §10.10's own table, which `AUDIT_rev43.md` found contradicted by three later owner-settled sections.
A4 200 mm VOIDS F330's PREMISE: F330's carried ceiling says in its own words that the 60–80 mm reasoning was "the ASKER's reasoning, offered in the option text, not a ruling he made." So the size is not ruled — but the CONDITION under which his yes was given (the roundel unresolvable) binds, and deleting the glyph restores that condition at ANY size. That is a stronger answer than shrinking.
A5 DOUBT ON THE COUNTER: fixed. No figures, no tolerances, no provenance grade on a child's card. The badge ceiling lives in `OPEN_FINDINGS.md`, where it already is.
A6 REGISTER STRADDLE: fixed, F331 children's line, declared.
A7 PAPEL PICADO: named. NOT re-asked — F330 forbids it — and its status is stated rather than silently dropped.
A8 THE DAYLIGHT OBJECT IS THE OBJECT: accepted, and it is now the point rather than a weakness.

DANGER (2/4, DANGER INTACT: FALSE) — the one lens that failed, and everything it prescribed is back. See the risk field for what I restored.

### CALENDARIO DE LA CASA — AÑO XXII, «YA VIENE»

**REGISTER** TWO REGISTERS, ALTERNATING, AND THE ALTERNATION IS THE HOUSE RULE THIS PROGRAMME HAS NEVER WRITTEN. **THE LINE CALENDAR IS WHAT WE KNOW; THE PAINTED CALENDAR IS WHAT IT WAS LIKE.** Odd years austere and model-drawn (the RECORD); even years polychrome and hand-painted in the Galas de México vernacular (the INVITATION). F331 line: ADULT, declared — it is free and handed to you, which F331 assigns to children, and I am claiming the exception because it hangs on a wall an adult chose. Precedents named on the colophon rather than discovered by a journalist: Galas de México, Jesús Helguera, Eduardo Cataño; Villalba, *Mexican Calendar Girls* (Chronicle, 2006); and Pirelli 1964– for the annual-commission thesis, which is a convention and not a discovery.  
**ARTEFACT** Giveaway wall calendar, 480 × 680 mm header board, tin strips top and bottom, 12-leaf tear-off block on 80 gsm offset, wire-stitched at the foot. Issued on 6 February — the day the house opened — and counted in years of the house.

**AÑO XXII (6 feb 2027 – 5 feb 2028) — THE LINE YEAR. THIS ONE SHIPS.** ONE ink: the measured body red 196/49/36, on warm uncoated 300 gsm. Its image window is the SIDE-ELEVATION LINE PASS off the model — 2711 strokes, 19471 points, 0 outside frame, watched printing this session. Serving flank, three equal apertures, counter, tail board, drip rail, wheels. **No livery on it:** the mural and the paisley are texture, not geometry, so a crease-and-contour pass cannot draw them, and the sheet draws nothing rather than approximating anything. That is SPEC §10.10 satisfied by construction instead of by argument. It composes the VW roundel out BY PROJECTION — a true side elevation cannot show a nose pressing — not by cropping and hoping.

**THE MASTHEAD IS NOT A GALAS MASTHEAD.** It is set from the house's own hand-lettered sign — FAVOR DE ORDENAR Y PAGAR AQUÍ, standing on the palm in `ref_rear34.jpg` in a beaded ochre frame. I cropped it and looked at it. It is the only piece of this business's own graphic design anywhere in the reference set, its four colours have sat measured in `playa_env.py` for revisions, and no artefact in 77 revisions has ever used them.

**DOWN THE LEFT MARGIN: TWENTY-ONE ROWS.** AÑO I through AÑO XXI, each with its span, each stamped NO EMITIDO. Twenty-one years of a business, and 77 revisions of this project, declared not issued — **on the face of the object, not hidden on the back.**

**AÑO XXIII (6 feb 2028) — THE PAINTED YEAR.** Six units, CMYK plus the two measured spots. Gated on a full year of the pilot's sell-through and on one owner ruling. Its picture is the terrace of `ref_rear34.jpg` at the moment BEFORE service: the woman at the formica, chin on her hand, waiting; napkin dispenser and salt shaker beside her; green gingham on the near table only; the second woman at the palm trunk, back to camera; the 118 festoon lamps lit warm. Its brief opens with one line: **ESTA NO ES UNA CHICA DE CALENDARIO. ESTÁ ESPERANDO SU COMIDA.**

A calendar is a machine for waiting. Twenty-one years of a business is waiting. Seventy-seven revisions that shipped nothing is waiting. And the photograph this object is built on is a picture of a woman waiting for a taco. One idea at three scales, and I did not invent it — I found it by opening the frame and looking, which is the only thing this project has ever been good at.

«YA VIENE» — *it's coming* — is what a waiter says to someone waiting. It is the masthead. It does the ethical work of the object in two syllables, ON the object, rather than in a risk note: the woman at the table is not on offer, she is waiting, and the house is telling her it's on its way.

**AÑO XXII SHIPS AND IT IS 100 % MODEL-DERIVED.** This is the inversion that answers the originality charge. The complaint against the painted calendar is that it uses the geometry least while costing most — the fifteen hexes are read off frames, the light key is a photograph-derived rig solve, and no viewer counts 118 lamps. True. So the year that ships first is the one where the model draws EVERY line on the sheet: the side elevation off `line_pass.py`, the twelve-leaf grid, the twenty-one NO EMITIDO rows generated from the opening date, and every figure read out of `STATE.md` at draw time with a refusal if it is missing. One ink. No illustrator. No spot colours. No factory minimum. No people, therefore no releases. It fires none of the four red flags the painted year fires, and it puts a real object in a customer's hand on the real anniversary — which establishes the series at NUMBER ONE instead of asking the owner to believe in number three.

**AND IT WRITES THE RULE NOBODY HAS WRITTEN.** The concept I was handed said "two house styles need a written rule about which is which, and nobody has written one," and then handed that to a future person — which, in a project whose rule 16 exists because unowned carriers get dropped, means the calendar quietly retires the austerity. Here the rule is the object's own structure: **odd years are the RECORD, even years are the INVITATION**, the pilot proves the first and pays for the second, and the alternation is printed in the colophon so the next context inherits it instead of re-deriving it.

**WHAT MAKES IT THIS BUS AND NOT A COMBI.** The masthead lettering comes off the house's own sign. The three apertures are equal at 515.5 × 403.0. The drip rail carries 118 lamps at a realised 28.5197 mm. The engine lid wears a chrome plate frame reading 1963. None of that can be borrowed by a rival taquería next month, because none of it is Volkswagen's — it is Tacombi's cut, Tacombi's sign-painter and Tacombi's electrician.

**THE RISK IT STILL CARRIES** — **WHAT I PUT BACK, AGAINST TWO AUDITORS' EXPLICIT ADVICE.**

**1. THE CALENDAR GIRL STAYS, AND SHE IS NOW THE POINT.** The danger lens said the risk had been replaced by a label. The fix for that is not to remove her. An American-invested restaurant group, in 2027, issuing a free calendar in the *chica de calendario* format — the format of the garage pin-up — with a young bare-shouldered woman at its centre. Held deliberately it is the best thing here: she is fully clothed, faintly bored, and WAITING TO BE FED, with a napkin dispenser and a salt shaker in frame. Held carelessly it is a taquería putting a customer on a pin-up calendar. **It can be read the second way by someone determined to, and no caption prevents that.** The inversion is stated in the brief with a failure state, and if the illustrator does not take it, the register is not usable and the concept dies rather than shipping sanded.

**2. HER FACE STAYS ON THE TABLE AS A BINARY, NOT A SOFTENING.** Both the feasibility and danger lenses offered the same escape — draw her from behind, three-quarters away, face occluded. That is precisely the "we'll generalise the faces" move this round was convened to condemn, wearing a better suit. So: either she is traced, asked, paid and **CREDITED BY NAME** in the foot line beside the address — which converts the exposure into the warmest thing on the sheet and is the only version that earns the documentary claim — or she is not traceable, she is painted turned toward the arriving plate, and the foot line prints the absence: **LA CLIENTA DE LA MESA · NO IDENTIFICADA.** Both ship a visible consequence. The quiet third option is named BANNED in the specification, not in a risk note, because nothing else stops it being the path of least resistance at commission time. **And I concede the odds: branch one is probably unavailable.** The reference set was ruled complete at rev 54 and PHOTOS_WANTED 1–5 are struck. Tracing a woman photographed on a Playa terrace around 2006 is harder than taking one new photograph, which has already been refused.

**3. NO EMITIDO STAYS AND MOVES TO THE FACE.** The owner-and-brand lens wants it off the object: *"nothing warm should carry a null result on its reverse."* I have done the opposite and put it on the front. Twenty-one years of a business declared not issued, on a gift, is bleak, and it will be the first thing someone softens to DESDE 2006 at print. It must not be. **The risk is real and I am naming it: this prints the company's own absence on the object it hands to strangers**, and a customer may simply read it as sad.

**4. FEBRUARY STAYS, WITH NO DECEMBER COMPANION.** The owner-and-brand lens offered a December year-card to remove risk 3. That kills the idea — Mexican businesses give calendars out in December, and replacing the civic new year with the house's own is the whole institutional arrogance of the object. **A share of customers and staff will call it broken and some will bin it in December.** The pilot exists partly to measure exactly that, empirically, which no owner ruling can.

**5. TRADE DRESS, UNRESOLVED.** A Volkswagen-shaped free giveaway, in the register of the historic Volkswagen de México dealer calendars, which also means it can read as VW-affiliated at ten feet — dilution running the other way. Named, gated on one page of advice, not solved.

**6. AND THE HONEST STRUCTURAL RISK: THE PAINTED YEAR MAY NEVER COME.** If the pilot sells the room and the painting never gets funded, this is a one-ink line calendar and the whole polychrome thesis was scaffolding. I think that is a survivable outcome. It is still a failure of the thing I said I was making.

**WHAT THE MODEL CONTRIBUTES** — **FOR AÑO XXII THE MODEL DRAWS EVERY LINE ON THE SHEET.** The image is `line_pass.py --view side` — 2711 strokes, 19471 points, watched. There is no photograph of the vehicle in true side orthographic projection: `ref_side.jpg` is a lens at 220 px/m with the cab door open and a man occluding 33 % of the front-wheel window. **A true elevation cannot be photographed; it can only be projected.** That is the one thing 366,490 faces and 0 non-manifold body edges uniquely buy, and it is the programme's own locked spine — *"vector line and flat colour."*

Three consequences follow from the projection rather than from taste, and each answers an audit finding for free:
* **The roundel is out of frame by geometry.** A side elevation cannot show a nose pressing. F191 stays shut and is untouched; nine rejections are not composed around, they are projected out.
* **§10.10 is satisfied by construction.** The line pass is crease-and-contour on GEOMETRY; the mural and paisley are TEXTURE. They cannot appear, so nothing is approximated and nothing is invented — the sheet simply has no livery and says so. *(Ceiling: I did not rasterise the bake to confirm zero artwork strokes. Do that before plate.)*
* **The twelve-leaf grid and the twenty-one NO EMITIDO rows are generated from the 6 February 2006 opening**, not typed, so the series cannot go stale by transcription — the defect this project pays for most often.

**FOR AÑO XXIII the model's contribution is a NAMED DELIVERABLE, not a justification** — which is the originality lens's fix 2, taken literally. The commission package is: (a) a print-size, geometrically correct line underlay from a camera composed to match `ref_rear34.jpg`'s own viewpoint — tail at frame right, serving flank to camera, nose off-frame; (b) a light card that READS SPEC §10.23 at draw time; (c) the BAKED artwork from `lid_gen.py` and `folk_gen.py` plus the source crops, as reproduction reference. Hand an illustrator that and the drawing is right before the first brush — **every VW bus in every calendar painting ever made is a caricature with the nose too round.** That sentence is the concept's best and it is the only originality claim the model can actually cash.

**AND ONE THING THE MODEL CANNOT DO, STATED AGAINST INTEREST.** F144: the festoon reads 4.96 and 3.82 in two photographs against the render's 1.38, both levers ablated, both failed. The single thing the customer's eye goes to is the thing the render loses, and a painting gets it for free. That is an argument for the painted year and quietly an argument that the asset is optional there — which is exactly why the year that ships first is the one where the asset is everything.

**MEASURED FIGURES** — **WATCHED PRINT THIS SESSION, ON HEAD (rev 77, `f06eb09`) — not transcribed:**

`BULB_X0 = T._aft(-1.8000) = -1.6368055555555556`; `BULB_X1 = 1.7000`; `BULB_PITCH = 0.0286`; span 3.3368055555555554 m; n = 117; **118 lamps at a realised pitch of 28.519705603038933 mm.** The round's own brief says 115 — the brief is wrong; cite `_aft()` so the next reader can recompute, because skipping that call gives 123.

`python3 line_pass.py --view side` → **2711 strokes / 19471 points, 0 outside frame, 1 checked 0 FAILED.** `--view hero34f` → **2065 strokes / 18581 points.** (An auditor reported 2063/18579 for the same view. Two different numbers for one bake: not run-to-run stable, or a different tree. Do not quote either as a constant; re-bake.)

Calendar arithmetic, derived not typed: **AÑO I = 6 feb 2006 – 5 feb 2007. AÑO XXI = 6 feb 2026 – 5 feb 2027** (current, seven months elapsed, unissuable). **AÑO XXII = 6 feb 2027 – 5 feb 2028. AÑO XXIII = 6 feb 2028 – 5 feb 2029.** 154 days to 6 feb 2027; 519 days to 6 feb 2028.

480 × 680 aspect 1.416667; A3 297 × 420 aspect 1.414141; **−0.178 %.** A true-proportion scale proof at 0.619× linear.

**READ OFF LIVE SOURCE (`playa_env.py`), the house's own sign — never used by any artefact:** `C_SIGN_FRAME` (208,177,111) #D0B16F, beaded rails · `C_SIGN_HEAD` (193,173,89) #C1AD59 · `C_SIGN_FIELD` (216,206,194) #D8CEC2 · `C_SIGN_INK` (70,62,50) #463E32 — the source's own comment: *"lettering — warm dark grey, NOT black."* Terrace: `C_VINYL` (195,110,68) · `C_GINGHAM_G` (143,173,127) · `C_GINGHAM_W` (175,199,164) *"NOT white (S4.4)"* · `C_LAMINATE` (195,167,112) · `C_TRUNK` (106,87,67).

**THE LIGHT KEY, WITH A CORRECTION.** `playa_env.py` prints flank : 72°-fascia as **report 3.95, rig 4.00**; foliage : cream **0.1093 against a 0.104–0.113 target**; L\* gap cream − foliage **52.6 against report 52.1**. I did NOT watch "12.5:1 palm trunk" or "0.93:1 up-facing cream" print, and the auditor that supplied them gave a *second, different* triple in its own fix ("3.95 / 1.87 / 0.772 beside 4.00 / 1.91 / 0.787"). **Two conflicting triples for one key is rule 5.** The light card must READ SPEC §10.23 at draw time and refuse if absent — never transcribe it.

**THE LAMPS, PROPERLY CITED (F144, `MEASURED-rev61`):** photograph `ref_side.jpg` **4.96** and `ref_rear34.jpg`'s aft coaming rail **3.82** against render **1.38** — 2.8–3.6× short, corroborated on two independent scenes. Both levers ablated and both failed: `T1_BULB_STR=60` moves it **1.40 → 0.88**, WORSE, because the bead clips toward white and clipped pixels are neutral; `T1_BULB_BASEV=0.30` moves **1.38 → 1.50**, nothing. **Drop "at V 0.95 the view transform takes the chroma out"** — that is an invented mechanism for a measured result. F144 also records the string runs the whole drip rail AND forms the serving-aperture surrounds (F112).

**FIGURES I REFUSE TO USE:** the 109.5/129.5 pillars (retracted; F336 re-derives 0.54σ, not 0.67σ); 1223.7 mm mural width (a function of `LID_OPEN_DEG`, bracketed 1187–1273); any counter height (`CNT_ZT 1.2540` is authored UN-DROPPED and the drop is station-dependent); the fringe ball count (derived, never printed).

**FIRST SHIPPABLE STEP** — **IN HIS HANDS THIS WEEK: THE PILOT ITSELF, AT A3, PRINTED ON AN OFFICE PRINTER FOR A FEW DOLLARS. NOT A MOCK-UP OF IT — IT.**

Write `calendario.py`, on the proven contract `sheet3_notissued.py` already established in this tree: every figure READ at draw time from `STATE.md`/`SPEC.md`/`playa_env.py`, and a **REFUSAL with a summary line naming what was missing** if any is absent (rule 37). **Write the kill first and watch it fail** (rule 3): `calendario.py --kill` removes one required figure and the script must refuse rather than draw a silently wrong sheet.

It draws, at 480 × 680 scaled to A3 — 0.619× linear, aspect error **−0.178 %** stated on the sheet:
1. the **side-elevation line pass** (`line_pass.py --view side`, 2711 strokes / 19471 points) in the image window;
2. the **12-leaf grid** for 6 feb 2027 – 5 feb 2028, generated from the opening date;
3. the **NO EMITIDO column**, AÑO I–XXI with spans, generated — not typed;
4. the **masthead** hand-traced from the house's own sign crop, `ref_rear34.jpg` (250,170)–(420,330), labelled a CRAFT CHOICE, not a measurement;
5. the **colophon** carrying the derivation — *AÑO I = 6 feb 2006 – 5 feb 2007; AÑO XXII = 6 feb 2027 – 5 feb 2028* — plus the precedents (Galas, Helguera, Cataño, Villalba; Pirelli for the series thesis) and the two-register rule in one sentence.

**NO NEW PRIMITIVE IS NEEDED. NO RENDER IS NEEDED. NO ILLUSTRATOR IS NEEDED. ONE INK.**

**THEN ONE QUESTION, WITH THE QUESTION TOOL, THE SHEET ATTACHED, AND THE GATING ITEM FIRST — not three safe questions with the unsafe one visible in the background.** The picture is set on your PLAYA terrace, which F92 deprioritised but did not cancel:

**(a)** Open the terrace. Line pilot AÑO XXII on 6 feb 2027; painted AÑO XXIII on 6 feb 2028.
**(b)** Line pilot only. Terrace stays closed. No painted year.
**(c)** Painted AÑO XXII for 6 feb 2027 — accept the compressed 5.1-month lead and ~$12–18k.
**(d)** Not this.

**And in the same message, one line, not multiple choice:** *may we see Tacombi's current brand identity?* — early critic 1's finding 9(d), 100 % unanswered across 75 concepts, and the masthead cannot be finished without it.

**Send him the same email as item 6 the same day** — two litho quotes, zero dollars — so that by the time he rules, the biggest number in the concept has stopped being an estimate.

**HOW IT ANSWERED THE FOUR LENSES** — **FEASIBILITY (2/4) attacked six things.** *Schedule gone* — right, 154 days against a self-stated 8-month lead; answered by splitting: line pilot at 154 days, painted year at 519. *Year number wrong* — right, and worse than stated: the masthead and the back panel could not both be true under either reading. I derived it and watched it print; AÑO XXII, with the rule in the colophon. *Likeness probably closed* — largely right; answered by making the pilot contain no people at all, so the object ships without a release, and the painted year's likeness question becomes a gate the owner rules on with a year of lead. *Three red flags fire* — the pilot fires none. *Step change in operating model* — the pilot is one digital printer. *`sheet.py` has no `img()` and is one-ink* — **and it does not need one**: the pilot's image IS line work, drawn with `line`/`poly`, so the blocker evaporates rather than being scheduled. *No camera composes the picture* — binds the PAINTED year only, and I go further than the fix: the camera should match the photograph, not `hero34f`.

**ORIGINALITY (1/4) landed the hardest blow and I concede it.** The Galas register is not available as a novelty and the Combi is the most-illustrated object in Mexican nostalgia graphics. It IS available as a VERNACULAR, which is different, and which a Mexican taquería is entitled to where a design studio quoting one is not — named on the colophon. Its three fixes are taken: **NO EMITIDO is promoted from backprint to the concept's structure and to the face**; the model is made LOAD-BEARING by making year one entirely line-drawn, which inverts "the concept that uses the geometry least costs the most"; and the Era-Fascista resemblance on ANNO XXI is cheaply defused — the Arabic span sits adjacent to the numeral always. Its fix 3 (paint the never-photographed off side) I decline for the PAINTING and accept for the LINE: `sheet3_notissued.py` already owns that subject, and duplicating it is the eight-way self-duplication the completeness critic condemned.

**OWNER-AND-BRAND (2.5/4) found the splice and it was real.** *Nolita cast in a Playa set* — answered by painting the window: the cast is TWO WOMEN, both Playa. *No §10.10 defence* — answered structurally for the pilot (nothing approximated, artwork absent and said to be absent) and contractually for the painted year (baked artwork + source crops as REPRODUCTION reference, not five swatches; measure the result back against the crop before plate). Its citation is upgraded per the completeness critic: the register row is **F94**, which records that the bar *"is in no live carrier AS A BAR"* and that four of seven named parts have no row at all — not §10.10's own contradicted table. *Roundel unaddressed* — killed by projection and declared. *Hook factually wrong* — right; SPEC's grade-S line is *"figured out how to cut open the Tacombi. Opened 6 February 2006."* The hook is now **el día que abrió**. *F331 unassigned* — assigned, adult line, exception declared with its reason. **I REJECT two of its fixes** (see risk).

**DANGER (2/4, DANGER INTACT: false) was correct and its finding is now the spine of the object.** I opened `ref_rear34.jpg` and looked. It is right: a young woman, bare-shouldered in an orange floral halter, chin on her hand, near-lens. In the Galas register, 480 × 680, given away free by a restaurant, that is a *chica de calendario* and the concept called it "kitsch." Its five attacks are all taken: the genre is NAMED on the object and in the brief; the inversion is SPECIFIED, not hoped for; the cast is re-cut from the frame; the fork is replaced with a binary that has a printed consequence either way and the quiet third option is named BANNED in the spec; the A3 now LEADS with the F92 gate; and the two-register rule is written here rather than assigned to nobody.

**EARLY CRITIC 1's TOP FINDING — the fabricated humans — binds this concept directly and I ran rule 8 on it.** Painted census, `ref_rear34.jpg`, published: **TWO people.** (1) Seated woman, near table, frame left — orange/yellow floral halter, bare shoulders, chin on right hand, left forearm on the formica, long dark hair, sandals, gaze a few degrees off the lens. (2) Standing woman at the palm trunk — back to camera, patterned top, grey-lilac skirt, white bag with dark pattern. **There is NO cook in a white cap and NO child in a magenta puffer in this frame.** The record's person in `ref_side.jpg` is a MAN IN A RED SHIRT (`AUDIT_rev12.md:868` — ceiling: cited, I did not open that frame). "Magenta puffer" is in no file: F220's three traced points on the child at the bumper are HAIR values, no garment, no colour. **Both struck.** *And one finding handed on:* the right end of `ref_rear34.jpg` is a red panel with a chrome plate frame reading 1963, a lock below and a round lamp — that reads as an ENGINE LID, i.e. the tail. SPEC §10.15 records the owner identifying this frame as a FRONT view. My crop does not corroborate it. Ceiling: one crop, ~250 px, JPEG. It changes no design decision — **there is no VW roundel anywhere in the frame either way.** Its other bindings: tree re-based to rev 77; brief's 115 lamps corrected to 118; F94 cited over §10.10 prose; no aperture rectangle; no 1223.7; no unstationed z.

**EARLY CRITIC 2's TOP FINDING — trade dress — is unanswered by the whole programme and I will not pretend otherwise.** A T1 silhouette given away free on 5,000 wall calendars is exposure, and 77 revisions contain zero IP analysis. One page of advice gates any run above the pilot. The side elevation with no roundel and no VW wordmark is the lowest-exposure composition available, which is a mitigation and not a clearance. Its *confession-tic* finding I answer by its own test — **name one design decision the confession changed**: NO EMITIDO changes the object's FORMAT, putting a 21-row column on the face and making a series-of-one rather than a one-off; and the UNMEASURED caption on the faces is what forces the likeness binary. **Every other printed admission is cut.** One confession, and it is structural. Its *no food in a food programme* finding: the pilot is food/people **FALSE** and I flag it false rather than claiming it.

---
## §3 ALL 60 VERDICTS, BY CONCEPT AND LENS

### DE MANO EN MANO — audit mean 2.25/4

**FEASIBILITY — 2/4 — FEASIBLE IN ITS FIRST STEP, UNPROVEN IN ITS CORE, AND MIS-COSTED IN ITS FINISH BY ROUGHLY AN ORDER OF MAGNITUDE.

What I verified and what holds. Every measured figure in this concept is real and correctly transcribed, which is better than most of this project's own briefs manage: CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860 (t1_detail.py, with the 0.182/0.191 two-frame corroboration in the comment above it); CNT_ZT, CNT_ZB = 1.2540, 1.1470 with the source's own "# 107 mm thick"; CNT_Y_IN, CNT_Y_OUT = 0.8450, 1.1660 "# 321 mm plan depth"; BAY_W = 0.5155 "# equal, measured"; Z_SILL, Z_HEAD, BAY_R = 1.3720, 1.7750, 0.0550 giving 403.0 mm and r 55; FRINGE_R, FRINGE_PITCH, FRINGE_INSET = 0.0090, 0.0260, 0.0130; "65 fringe balls per aperture" is the source's own phrase; emit=(1.000, 0.918, 0.790); plancha top 1.455 with y -0.460..0.100. The verbatim quote "a serving hatch shows you its strip light" is a real comment in t1_detail.py. And the ink catch is genuine and load-bearing: lid_gen.py reads INK = (72, 46, 6) # strip lettering; measured median (91,59,7) is..., and la_rueda.py line 173 — the artefact rev 77 actually SHIPPED — uses (72, 46, 6). This concept caught a live error in a shipped deliverable. Credit that.

One thing is better than the concept knew: the interior is not a hollow of back-faces. t1_detail.py states "The body is solidified to a 2.8 mm shell, so the underside of the surviving roof strips either side of the opening is already real geometry carrying the body material -- which IS the inside of the red exterior paint." An interior camera has real surfaces to see.

Now the attacks.

1. THE INFRASTRUCTURE ARGUMENT IS SPENT AND THE GREP CLAIM IS NOW FALSE. "I GREPPED EVERY .py IN THIS TREE: THERE IS NO FREESTYLE, NO GREASE PENCIL, NO AO PASS, NO CRYPTOMATTE." /home/user/combi_render/line_pass.py exists — 278 lines, Grease Pencil Line Art to polylines to SVG, shipped at rev 77, and its own docstring already dismantles the rev-76 grep that this concept is repeating ("as written that is a BASIC-regex grep, so | is a literal pipe and it searches for one impossible string"). So "building it is shared infrastructure with the keystone line item, so this concept pays for something else too" no longer buys anything — rev 77 already paid. And line_pass emits polylines, not occlusion, so the AO pass this concept actually wants is still unbuilt and now has no co-funder. Meanwhile the concept's own fallback (an inverted 96-spp render) makes the AO pass optional. Net: the model contribution collapses to a trim size plus a reference image any camera could have supplied. That does not stop it shipping — it makes it the cheapest thing in the bench — but the cost line rests on a co-funding argument that is dead.

2. THE COMPOSITION HAS NEVER BEEN RENDERED AND THE BUILT GEOMETRY ARGUES AGAINST IT. This is the biggest one. The galley fitout is packed into y -0.50..+0.10: GAL_Y_BACK = -0.4800 (backdrop slab -0.492..-0.468), gal_worktop y -0.44..-0.18, utensil rail_y = -0.3600, gal_tube y -0.182..-0.058, plancha y -0.460..+0.100. The flank sits at y ~ +0.836 and the counter is cantilevered outboard of it at 0.845..1.166. To put the plancha BETWEEN the camera and the aperture — which is what "Behind, the plancha" requires — the camera must sit at y < -0.46, i.e. inside or behind a solid backdrop slab. There is no room. Put the camera in the clear space (y +0.1..+0.84) and the plancha, the strip light, the worktop and the rail are all behind it. The four-element composition the ARTEFACT paragraph describes — counter across the bottom, fringe at the cut edge, strip light top left, plancha behind — is not obviously constructible, and no frame exists to settle it. The concept calls the camera "cheap" without having watched it print (rule 5) and describes an image it has not looked at (rule 1), in the one programme whose authority is that it looks.

3. IT LANDS ON THE OLDEST UNTOUCHED ROW IN THE REGISTER. OPEN_FINDINGS.md F14: "gal_end_f sees past its own end. Needs its OWN sight line -- do not inherit REAR_W/2", OPEN, 260.0 mm show side / 20.0 mm off side, INHERITED-rev52 — un-re-measured for about 25 revisions. The galley's end returns exist specifically "to stop a camera seeing past the backdrop into unlit body cavity," and one of them is known to fail. Every camera to date has looked at the galley from outside through a 515 mm hole. The first camera to stand inside is the first to test F14 from the inside. The honest budget is not "one camera, cheap"; it is "one camera, one render, look, then an unknown amount of interior geometry" — which is exactly the open-ended work the owner is impatient about.

4. THE INTERIOR HAS NO PHOTOGRAPHIC GROUNDING AT ALL. The 16 checksummed reference frames are exteriors. There is no interior photograph of this vehicle in the reference set; PHOTOS_WANTED items 1-5 are ruled "not possible now"; the reference set has been frozen since rev 54. The concept concedes "galley Y is authored everywhere in the source, by the source's own admission." So the one choice that distinguishes this concept from everything else in the bench — the point of view — sits wholly inside the authored region. A print whose declared virtue is "the frame the hands cross is true" is putting its true frame around a view nobody has ever seen.

5. THE PRINTING LABOUR IS UNDERSTATED BY ROUGHLY 10x. "Hand-printing 300 give-aways on newsprint is an afternoon" — 300 copies x 3 blocks is 900 baren pulls, plus re-registration on every pass, plus inter-colour drying. Oil-based relief ink on 60 gsm newsprint needs hours to overnight between passes; colour 2 into wet colour 1 offsets, and 60 gsm cockles and tears on re-registration. Realistic: three sessions over 4-5 days, 12-20 hours of hand labour. The signed 50 on 250 gsm rag is worse — rag at that weight wants damping and press pressure; a baren will not pull it well. "or small press" is named and is not in the US$120. And the headline "$120 + $9" sits beside "a commissioned printmaker ... two to three days' work — quote it," which for three registered blocks carrying two hands, a tray, ~65 bobbles and 62 characters of reversed text is realistically 4-6 days and US$1,500-3,500. The reader takes away $130; the finished artefact is $2,000-plus.

6. IT NEEDS MORE PHYSICAL ACCESS THAN ANY OTHER CONCEPT IN A PROJECT THAT CANNOT OBTAIN A PHOTOGRAPH. "Put them on the tables Friday with the check" needs a service floor, staff and the restaurant's permission. Stated risk (5) — "the food must be drawn from THIS restaurant's actual plate, in front of the cook" — needs kitchen-side access. The record: CLAUDE.md, "He has never stood in the vehicle"; HANDOFF_rev24, "That owner is the restaurant's owner, not Donald. Donald has never stood in the bus"; PHOTOS_WANTED 1-5 "not possible now." A programme that cannot get one new photograph of a bus is being asked to get a cook to plate food for a life-drawing session. The gate is self-imposed and no fallback is supplied.

7. THE ONE THING THAT CAN KILL IT HAS NO GATE. Everything else here is checkable against the machine — sheet3_notissued.py reads 40 figures out of STATE.md at draw time and refuses if a regex misses; la_rueda goes through the line pass; probes refuse when inputs are absent. This artefact's declared virtue is "cut by a human hand," and its stated position is that wobble, slip and a reversed letter all stay in. Defensible as aesthetics; fatal as quality control. A badly drawn hand and a beautifully drawn hand produce the same PASS. Hands are the hardest subject in figurative drawing, 62 characters of reversed 6-8 mm lettering is the highest-slip element in the bench, the block is irreversible, and "the wobble is the point" is unfalsifiable cover for bad craft.

Also, a smaller inconsistency worth naming: the FIRST STEP says "working over an inverted print of an existing render," but every existing render is an exterior (front, side, hero34f, hero34r) and out/ starts empty on a clone besides. So the first step either uses a frame that is not the described composition, or it silently depends on item 2 being settled first.

WHAT SURVIVES. Compute cost is genuinely near zero — one render, one camera entry in studio.py's VIEWS, no queue, no fan-out, no 20-minute bootstrap, and a perspective camera does dodge the front/rear ortho clipping outright. Relief printing is a real formal match to an interior-out subject: you cut away the light, and the light is the aperture, so the key block is mostly ink with the hole reversed out. And the first step is the cheapest falsifiable test in the whole bench — $9, one block, one ink, 30 spoon-pulls, thirty customers voting. That is what the owner asked for when he said "we need to finally present something."**

* ATTACKS: 1. GREP CLAIM FALSE / CO-FUNDING SPENT — line_pass.py (278 lines, Grease Pencil Line Art, rev 77) exists; its own docstring already refutes the rev-76 grep this concept repeats. "Building it pays for the keystone too" buys nothing now. line_pass emits polylines, not occlusion, so the AO pass is still unbuilt with no co-funder — and the concept's own fallback makes it optional, reducing the model contribution to a trim size plus a reference image.

2. THE COMPOSITION IS UNRENDERED AND THE GEOMETRY FIGHTS IT — fitout packed into y -0.50..+0.10 (backdrop -0.480, worktop -0.44..-0.18, rail -0.360, tube -0.182..-0.058, plancha -0.460..+0.100) against a flank at ~+0.836. To get the plancha between camera and aperture the camera must sit at y < -0.46, i.e. inside the backdrop slab. Put it in the clear space and plancha, strip light, worktop and rail are all behind it. Four-element composition not shown constructible; rules 1 and 5 both broken.

3. F14 — OPEN_FINDINGS.md, "gal_end_f sees past its own end," 260.0 mm show side / 20.0 mm off side, INHERITED-rev52, ~25 revisions un-re-measured. The end returns exist "to stop a camera seeing past the backdrop into unlit body cavity" and one is known to fail. The first camera ever placed inside tests it from the inside. "One camera, cheap" is an unwatched figure.

4. NO INTERIOR PHOTOGRAPH EXISTS — all 16 checksummed refs are exteriors; PHOTOS_WANTED 1-5 ruled not possible; reference set frozen since rev 54. The concept's single distinguishing choice (the POV) sits wholly in authored geometry, which it concedes.

5. PRINTING LABOUR UNDERSTATED ~10x — 300 x 3 blocks = 900 baren pulls plus per-pass re-registration plus inter-colour drying on 60 gsm newsprint that cockles and offsets. Not "an afternoon": 3 sessions over 4-5 days, 12-20 h. The 250 gsm rag edition of 50 needs a press that is not in the $120. Headline reads "$130"; commissioned three-block cutting of two hands, a tray, ~65 bobbles and 62 reversed characters is 4-6 days and US$1,500-3,500.

6. ACCESS IT HAS NEVER HAD — tables-on-Friday needs floor access; "drawn in front of the cook" needs kitchen access. CLAUDE.md: "He has never stood in the vehicle." HANDOFF_rev24: "That owner is the restaurant's owner, not Donald." A project that cannot obtain one photograph is being asked for a plating session. No fallback offered.

7. THE FAILURE MODE IS UNGATEABLE — every other artefact here is checked against the machine (sheet3 reads 40 STATE.md figures at draw time and refuses on a missed regex). This one's virtue is the hand, and its position is that slips stay in. A bad hand and a good hand both PASS. Hands are the hardest subject; 62 reversed 6-8 mm characters is the highest-slip element in the bench; the block is irreversible.

8. FIRST STEP SELF-CONTRADICTS — "working over an inverted print of an existing render," but every existing render is an exterior and out/ starts empty on a clone. Either the proof is not the described image, or the step silently depends on attack 2 being resolved first.

VERIFIED AND CREDITED: all ~10 quoted constants check out in t1_detail.py / t1_shell.py; the (91,59,7) vs (72,46,6) catch is real (lid_gen.py:359) and catches a live error in the SHIPPED la_rueda.py:173; the 2.8 mm solidified shell means the interior has real surfaces, which is better than the concept claimed.
* WOULD FIX IT: a. RENDER THE VIEW BEFORE ANYTHING ELSE — 30 MINUTES, AND IT DECIDES THE CONCEPT. Add one perspective camera to studio.py's VIEWS dict, render one frame, crop it, look. Settle whether counter + fringe + strip light + plancha can co-exist given the fitout in y -0.50..+0.10 against a flank at +0.836. Publish the camera's (loc, target, lens) alongside the concept so it is reproducible.
