It also collides with three items already on the bench — THE TOY THAT WAS NEVER MADE (the surveyed-proportions argument verbatim), TRES HUECOS (the apertures as the mark), ENGINE: NONE (the motor line) — so what remains after stripping them is a cavity. On the second charge: the register is genuinely new, the move is not — divide a measured figure by 20, mould the quotient underneath, print the unresolved question on the face, confess the omission. That is THE UNCERTAINTY PLATE in polypropylene, the third instance of confession-as-content, and the only graphic design in a graphic-design programme is three lines of microtype on an underside nobody reads.**

* ATTACKS: 1. PRIOR ART, DENSEST FIRST. (a) The Mexican clay whistle is not a niche — it runs continuously from Teotihuacan and Maya bird whistles to today's mercado stalls; naming the piece SILBATO plants it inside that tradition and then hands it to injection-moulded polypropylene, the version that loses the comparison. (b) Vehicle/animal ocarinas fingered through the object's own features are the traditional solution, not a fresh one. (c) VW T1 memorabilia is the world's most merchandised vehicle; ceramic VW ocarinas already exist in Latin American markets. (d) Promotional logo whistles are a commodity line item; Mexican restaurants handing children whistles and maracas is the most predictable object in the space. (e) "The bus lost its engine sound" is exhausted ID.Buzz campaign territory.

2. BENCH DUPLICATION, THREE TITLES. The surveyed-proportions argument IS "THE TOY THAT WAS NEVER MADE". The fingering IS "TRES HUECOS". The MOTOR: NINGUNO line IS "ENGINE: NONE". SILBATO reads as three bench titles fused and given a mouthpiece.

3. THE HEADLINE CLAIM IS FALSE. "Every VW bus toy ever made is a caricature... the first one whose proportions were surveyed rather than remembered" — Tamiya's 1:24 Type 2, Schuco, Minichamps and the licensed 1:18 models are built from factory drawings and CAD under VW licence. The defensible claim is far smaller: first object of THIS converted vehicle. 1:20 is also a non-standard scale that signals "not a collector's object" to exactly the audience that would check.

4. THE TWO HEADLINE CLAIMS CANCEL. An ocarina requires a windway and a sharp-edged voicing window in addition to the fingering holes. The concept names neither. Wherever they go they are an un-surveyed protrusion and a slot cut in the body, so "203.3 x 87.5 straight off the mesh, nothing added" and "it is an ocarina" cannot both be true of one object. This, not the STL exporter, is the real gate.

5. THE ACOUSTICS ARE PROBABLY UNMUSICAL, NOT MERELY UNMEASURED. Lumped Helmholtz, 2 mm wall, flanged end correction, cavity bracketed 250-900 cm3 because nobody has computed V off the mesh (watched print, /tmp/silbato.py): three holes open 359-681 Hz, two open 323-612, one open 268-508. Three-open to one-open is a 951-cent interval — all three fingers buy not quite an octave in two lumpy steps. That is a warbling siren, not a fingering. And "cover all three and it sings low" resolves to the voicing window alone; at a plausible 80 mm2 that reads 158-301 Hz, an absurd note for an 80 mm cavity that will most likely refuse to speak. CEILING: lumped model, no measured V, no windway geometry — unsettlable without blowing one.

6. THE DISCLAIMER IS NOT A DEFENCE. Omitting the roundel does not clear T1 trade dress; VW licenses that silhouette precisely for toys. "EMBLEM OMITTED, NOT LICENSED BY VOLKSWAGEN" moulded into the part is a confession stamped on the evidence. The reason nobody has made an unlicensed measured VW bus toy is not that nobody thought of it.

7. OLD AESTHETIC, NEW LABEL. The measurement fetish survives the change of register intact: a quotient moulded underneath, an unresolved question printed on the face, a confession about what was omitted. Third instance of the house move after THE UNCERTAINTY PLATE and NO LEGIBLE.
* WOULD FIX IT: 1. MOVE THE MOUTHPIECE TO WHERE THE ENGINE ISN'T. Blow into the engine bay; voice it through the louvres/louvbay slots already on the mesh, or the roof_vent. The windway stops being a foreign object and becomes the thesis — MOTOR: NINGUNO made literal with the child's own mouth. This repairs the contradiction in item 4 and is the one move in the concept that nobody has made.

2. MAKE IT BARRO, NOT POLYPROPYLENE. Slip-cast from the mesh via a plaster master, made by a named whistle workshop (Ocumicho, Metepec, Santa Maria Atzompa). The collision of a 366,490-face survey with a hand tradition is the fresh part; generic promo plastic is the part that has been done. Stamp the maker's name instead of the division sum. Watch glaze-lead and small-parts testing.

3. DROP THE CLAIM TO WHAT IS TRUE. "The first object of this converted bus", not "the first accurate VW combi". Which means the artefact must carry the conversion — three apertures, counter line, tail board, hatch — not a generic T1. This also thins the trade-dress exposure.

4. FIX THE FINGERING OR ABANDON IT. 513 mm2 a hole is far too much area against any plausible cavity. Either shrink the sounding hole to ~60-90 mm2 inside the moulded aperture outline (the aperture stays true at 25.775 x 20.150; the hole inside it is the instrument), or go to 1:12 — body 338.8 x 145.8 mm, aperture 42.96 x 33.58, 1424.7 mm2 — where a real cavity and windway fit. Say which, and why, on the packet.

5. CHANGE THE MOULDED LINE TO ONE THE HOUSE HAS NEVER PRINTED. Not a quotient — the note the object actually made, stamped per batch after firing, because clay and fire move it. Measurement as consequence rather than measurement as decoration, which is the only version of the house move that dodges charge 7.

6. TEST FOR NOTHING, THIS WEEK, BEFORE ANY CODE. Two clay or card cavities and one windway, blown by hand. Write the 359-681 Hz prediction down FIRST, then measure, then report which way the estimate was wrong. The STL exporter is worth writing for the programme, but it is not this concept's gate and treating it as the gate hides item 4.

7. STATE ON THE FACE which of THE TOY THAT WAS NEVER MADE / TRES HUECOS / ENGINE: NONE this supersedes or merges with, or it will be screened as a repackage of all three.

8. DISTRIBUTION. Not 200 whistles at the door. With a child's meal only, or sold — and make the PACKET the graphic-design deliverable, which is where this programme actually gets an artefact out of the idea.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

**OWNER-AND-BRAND — 1/4 — REJECT AS WRITTEN. This is the only artefact on the bench that subtracts every asset Tacombi owns and keeps only the asset Volkswagen owns — and it does it in three dimensions, as a product, for children. A bare unpainted polypropylene shell with no mural, no Señor Tacombi script, no Calidad sunburst, no menu strips, no colour and a deliberately blank nose is not a Tacombi combi. It is a VW T1. SPEC 10.10's own stated purpose is "the bar is that the owner recognises HIS OWN VEHICLE"; the concept passes 10.10's letter by the rule-34 argument (correctly — I concede that reasoning is the best on the bench) and fails 10.10's purpose completely. He would hand a child his restaurant's giveaway and the child would be holding somebody else's brand. That is the brand-fit core and it is not a trim.**

* ATTACKS: 1. THE INVERSION. Strip mural, script, Calidad, roundel, menu strips and all colour and what survives is the T1 silhouette — the one part of this vehicle that is not his. Every other concept on the bench at least carries his artwork or his cut. This one carries only the donor marque. A taqueria's door gift that advertises Volkswagen is dilution of his brand by his own hand.

2. THE MOULDED DISCLAIMER MAKES IT WORSE, NOT SAFER. "EMBLEM OMITTED, NOT LICENSED BY VOLKSWAGEN" cast into the part is not a shield; it is a permanent, physical, dated record that the maker knew there was a mark and proceeded. rev-77 CRITIC 1 already named this class — "every commercially significant item here is a Volkswagen T1 silhouette or roundel, and in 77 revisions there is not one line about trade dress" — and a 203 mm three-dimensional scale replica is the most exposed possible instance of it, far more than any poster. Removing the roundel does nothing when the body shape is the thing being sold; VW licenses toy Bulli bodies precisely because the shape is the asset. Worse, the pitch markets on that accuracy: "the first accurately-proportioned VW combi anybody has ever made" is a claim to have replicated another company's product form better than its licensees. CEILING, STATED: I am not counsel and the actual exposure is unmeasured and not recoverable from what we hold — but the programme's most exposed item should not be the one that also has the least of his brand on it.

3. THE MARKET CLAIM IS AN UNMEASURED FIGURE, AND IT INVITES THE COMPARISON THIS MODEL LOSES. "Every VW bus toy ever made is a caricature: nose too round, roof too tall, arches wrong" is a claim about a market nobody surveyed — a claim in prose, not a measurement, in a project whose first rule forbids exactly this. And it stakes the object's whole value on survey supremacy while the record says: the nose is the owner's second named defect, the emblem rejected NINE times, gloss 0.412 against a 0.60 bar, the front and rear ortho cameras CLIP, the tail board sits at 38.0° built against 28.0° photographed (unresolved seven revisions), the rear hatch is a declared 64° POSE that was never measured, and the OFF SIDE elevation is graded E, never photographed. A poster can crop a grade-E side away. A hand-held object in the round cannot — this would be the first artefact in the programme that commits the un-photographed half of the vehicle to a physical surface, and a collector will hold it beside a Schuco and judge it with an eye, not a probe.

4. ITS OWN STATED SIZE IS WRONG-SHAPED. "203.3 × 87.5 mm" is LENGTH × WIDTH — a plan footprint labelled as the object. The height is absent: roof at 1:20 is 99.2 mm, 156.6 mm to bbox top with the lids and tail board up. Height is the dimension that decides mouth geometry, hand fit and whether the tail board is a snappable projection. Omitting it in the one paragraph that exists to prove the object is too big to swallow is a figure defect in a safety argument.

5. THE CHEAP PATH AND THE SAFE PATH ARE NOT THE SAME PATH, AND THE CONCEPT PRESENTS THEM AS ONE. "Fifty whistles in a restaurant for the price of a night's covers" cannot legally happen before the CPSIA/F963 testing the document prices as later — and an FDM print glued from two halves is the worst possible regulated article: layer lines, an adhesive joint, unknown filament, no lot traceability, and the glue joint is precisely the detached-mouthpiece recall mode the concept itself cites. So the "cheap path is real" claim is not a step toward the safe path; it is a different, unshippable path.

6. THE RISK IS ASYMMETRIC IN A WAY TESTING CANNOT FIX, AND IT IS A FOOD RISK. Upside: a charming door gift. Downside: a product-safety story with a restaurant's name on it. A food business cannot absorb that, because it contaminates the core proposition — people stop trusting the kitchen, not the toy. And "a shared mouthpiece in a restaurant is a conversation the front of house will have, not the designer" is the tell: the designer is handing away the single question that decides whether the object exists.

7. IT IS THE ADULT LINE'S VOICE WEARING THE CHILDREN'S LINE'S BODY, IN THE REVISION WHERE HE RULED THOSE TWO LINES APART. F331 (RULED-rev77): "Split it deliberately — kids get one line, adults another… neither pretends to be the other." Every unit of wit here is adult: MOTOR: NINGUNO is a joke about a gutted vehicle; deadpan moulded catalogue type on the underside; a printed confession that the acoustics are unmeasured; a trademark disclaimer. A six-year-old gets a grey plastic bus. A design-literate adult gets the whole piece. That is the exact pretence F331 just forbade.

8. IT ANSWERS A QUESTION HE DID NOT ASK, IN THE REVISION HE ANSWERED A DIFFERENT ONE. F330 (RULED-rev77): "Yes — start it now" — F18, the die-cut sticker, unblocked for the first time since rev 44. F18 is free, for children, "should spark joy and be something families keep", with an owner-LOCKED style, a LOCKED scene, a named audience and a written spec in AUDIT_rev43.md §5. The whistle competes for the identical moment (a free thing handed to a child at the door) and the identical budget line, while costing 4,000–9,000 USD of tooling, 500–1,500 USD of compulsory lab testing per SKU, and carrying regulatory and trademark exposure the sticker does not have — and it arrives in the very revision where he said start the sticker. "IT IS NOT F18" is conceded in the document but never reckoned with.

9. THE TWO-DAY PROMISE SHIPS A MUTE GREY LUMP TO A MAN WHO SAID "we need to finally present something". The concept's own most likely failure is that 513 mm² a hole will not speak, and its fallback is to hand him the object anyway and call the negative a result. He has just spent rev 75 being handed a revision that only measured, and F330/F331 exist because he finally got something in his hand. Handing him an unpainted, unbranded, silent plastic bus and explaining that the number was 513 is rev 75 in polypropylene. Meanwhile the "half a day of code" STL exporter is an estimate in a repository where every half-day instrument estimate has been wrong for seventy-seven revisions.

WHAT I CONCEDE, BECAUSE IT IS REAL. The trademark handling is more candid than anything else on the bench and it names its own blocker precisely. It refuses the forbidden pillar asymmetry explicitly. It prints its unmeasured claim on the face of the artefact, which is this project's house style done right. Its arithmetic reproduces — I recomputed 512.9 mm²/hole, 1538.6 mm² total and +45.2 cents from 20 °C to 35 °C, and all three print. The three-equal-apertures fingering is the strongest measured fact this project owns, honestly used. And SOUND was a named-absent register. The idea underneath is good. The body it is wrapped in is the problem.
* WOULD FIX IT: STOP MAKING A BUS. Take rev-77 CRITIC 1's own pivot, which is already on the bench: TRES HUECOS. The three serving apertures are Tacombi's cut, not Volkswagen's pressing.

1. RE-BODY IT. The whistle is not a T1. It is a simple non-vehicle volume — a rounded slab or bar, the proportion taken from the counter nosing or the aperture band — carrying the three measured openings at 515.5 × 403.0, r55, in the measured pillar rhythm, scaled to whatever aperture size actually sounds. Same three-note fingering, same honest measured figures moulded underneath, same "MOTOR: NINGUNO" line if he likes it. What changes: zero VW silhouette, zero roundel question, zero disclaimer needed, and the mark it advertises is HIS. This also deletes attacks 1, 2, 3 and 4 in one move and makes the object about the taqueria rather than the donor vehicle. It gets stronger, not safer — a mark with no vehicle in it at all is the braver object.

2. ANSWER THE PHYSICS BEFORE WRITING THE EXPORTER. The whole idea stands or falls on whether that opening ratio speaks, and that question does not need an STL, a mesh, or a line of repo code. Build the cavity in card or a stopped tube at the same internal volume and same 1538.6 mm² total open area and blow it — an afternoon, zero cost. If it will not sound, you have the result before you have spent the revision, and you print the working aperture scale on the underside next to the true one exactly as the concept already proposes. Only write the STL export once the acoustics say yes.

3. PICK ONE LINE AND SAY WHICH (F331 requires it). Either (a) ADULT: a counter object or keyring, sold not given, no F963/EN71 regime at all, and F18's sticker carries the children's line the owner just unblocked; or (b) CHILD: then the lab test is a GATE BEFORE the 50-piece trial, not a later cost; single-piece moulding only, no glued halves (that is the recall mode it cites); mouth end oversized well past the small-parts cylinder; and one per child taken home, never a bowl at the door. Do not present the printed trial as a legal step toward (b) — it is not one.

4. MAKE THE FIRST OBJECT NOT GREY. Print it in the measured menu-strip yellow (172,144,17) or the body red so that if it is mute it still reads as something of his. A silent coloured object is a prototype; a silent grey object is a failure he can hold.

5. DELETE THE MARKET CLAIM OR MEASURE IT. Either strike "the first accurately-proportioned VW combi anybody has ever made", or buy three commercial 1:20-ish T1 toys, measure them against the mesh and print the deltas. And carry the object's own accuracy ceiling in the house style: tail board 38.0 built against 28.0 photographed, hatch 64.0 a declared pose, off side graded E — an object in the round cannot crop those away and should say so.

6. IF HE INSISTS ON THE BUS SHAPE, make the trademark opinion a named, priced gate before any tooling, and take the disclaimer OUT of the mould — a cast line naming the mark documents knowledge and cures nothing. Put it in the question to him, not in the part.

7. ASK HIM ONE QUESTION, WITH THE OBJECT IN FRONT OF HIM, MULTIPLE CHOICE: (a) a three-hole Tacombi whistle with no vehicle in it, free, for children; (b) the accurate 1:20 bus as an adult keepsake, sold; (c) neither — put it into F18's sticker, which you just unblocked; (d) sticker first, whistle after. He is a restaurateur: (c) and (d) are live answers and the concept must survive being told so.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

**DANGER — 3/4 — DANGER LARGELY INTACT, BUT WITH THREE SPECIFIC SANDINGS — and the sharpest material available is sitting unused one file away. The mournful core survives because it is MOULDED INTO THE OBJECT, not printed on a card: "MOTOR: NINGUNO", "TRANSMISSION: SOLD", "EMBLEM OMITTED, NOT LICENSED BY VOLKSWAGEN". Those three lines under a child's toy ARE the concept, and they are content rather than warning label — the failure mode I was sent to find (the herb in the chipped tin) has not happened to them. The regulatory exposure, the shared mouthpiece, and "it may not speak and we will print that it didn't" are named without being defused. That is why this scores above the middle. What HAS been sanded: the wager has a trapdoor, the artefact is stripped of everything that makes it THIS bus, and the distribution surrenders before anyone objects. Score 3, and it is two edits from 4.**

* ATTACKS: WHAT IS GENUINELY DANGEROUS HERE, NAMED FIRST SO THE JUDGEMENT IS AUDITABLE:

1. A funerary object handed to a child. The vehicle was gutted — the air-cooled flat-four, the most recognisable noise ever fitted to a road car, sold for parts. You hand a child the corpse and it sings. Mournful, and it is on the object in moulded type. INTACT.
2. Trade dress. A 1:20 accurate VW T1 body IS Volkswagen's protected trade dress. Omitting the roundel does not cure that; VW licenses die-cast aggressively and has litigated shape. So "EMBLEM OMITTED, NOT LICENSED BY VOLKSWAGEN" is not a shield — it is a signed ADMISSION moulded permanently into a children's product. That is MORE dangerous than the concept realises, and the concept presents it as prophylaxis. INTACT BUT MIS-LABELLED: do not let a later pass "improve" this into a safety measure.
3. Regulated article. CPSIA / ASTM F963 / EN 71, mouth contact, whistle recall history for detached mouthpieces, third-party testing that can FAIL and costs real money. Named, priced, not trimmed. INTACT.
4. Two hundred children with whistles in his dining room. Named as a fact. SURRENDERED as a position — see sanding 3.
5. The charge nobody wrote down: a Mexican street-food working vehicle, immobilised, reproduced as a party favour handed to children on their way out of a restaurant. The taquero's tool as a souvenir. Set against "MOTOR: NINGUNO / TRANSMISSION: SOLD" that is the most uncomfortable thing available anywhere in this brief, and it is ABSENT. Not sanded — never picked up.

THE THREE SANDINGS:

SANDING 1 — THE TRAPDOOR. This is the bad one. The concept's whole honest claim is "the fingering is this vehicle's single most solid fact ... three holes, three notes, EQUAL BECAUSE THE MESH SAYS EQUAL." Then: "If the body will not sound at true scale, the apertures get a scale of their own and THAT number goes on the underside." That sentence converts a wager into a design task. The moment the holes may be 0.6x or 1.4x they are no longer the measured serving apertures, the mesh is no longer the instrument, and the object is an ocarina with a bus on it — precisely the caricature toy the concept opens by attacking. You cannot claim "surveyed, not remembered" and reserve the right to resize the survey. 513 mm2 a hole is enormous beside any ocarina fingering and the concept knows it; the honest position is that this probably does NOT speak at true scale and the object is a bet on a number. An escape hatch is a sanded edge.

SANDING 2 — THE OBJECT HAS BEEN SHAVED BACK TO A SHAPE. "No paint, no decal" erases the Senor Tacombi script, the folk-art mural, the Calidad sunburst, the 115-lamp festoon — every hand-made, un-factory thing about this vehicle, i.e. everything that makes it THIS bus rather than any T1. It is presented as austerity. It also very conveniently dissolves the SPEC 10.10 artwork-replication fight by omission and then takes credit for saying so in moulded type. A blank natural-polypropylene bus does not "spark joy for children" (F18's own bar); it is a supermarket bath toy.

SANDING 3 — PRE-SURRENDER. "Given at the door on the way OUT, and if he says no it is dead, correctly." The concept concedes its own distribution before anyone has objected. Its premise is that a silenced vehicle's noise is returned; the CONSEQUENCE of that premise is that two hundred children make a noise nobody chose, in his room. That is the point, not the flaw. Volunteering the veto in advance is the polite version of the idea.

TWO FACTUAL BITES THE CONCEPT MISSES — BOTH WOULD MAKE IT MORE DANGEROUS, NOT LESS. Both verified in the tree this session:

* SPEC.md line 138: "| Side, off side (-Y) | twin outward-hinged cargo doors + three glazed windows in the same bay positions | **E** (never photographed) |". A whistle is three-dimensional and has two flanks. This object moulds, permanently, at 1:20, an entire side of a vehicle that NO photograph in the reference set shows. "Surveyed rather than remembered" is therefore HALF TRUE and the concept does not say which half.
* THE POSE PROBLEM. t1_shell.py, above TB_TILT_DEG: "*** DO NOT 'FIX' THIS TO 28.0 ... THIS BOARD IS HINGED: ITS ANGLE IS A POSE, NOT A DIMENSION ***", with ref_side 38.8 deg, IMG_3840 21.0 deg, and "THE TWO FRAMES DISAGREE BY 18 DEGREES BECAUSE SOMEBODY PROPPED IT DIFFERENTLY ON TWO DIFFERENT DAYS." Plus REAR_OPEN_DEG = 64.0, a declared pose no frame shows. A mould cannot be revised. This object freezes one afternoon's prop angle into thousands of identical copies forever. That is the strongest thing available to this concept and it is not in it. (Note also that the incoming framing "tail board 38 vs 28, unresolved for seven revisions" is STALE — the repo resolved it at rev 70 and the resolution is better material than the dispute was.)
* Minor but it is the project's own rule 5: "the first accurately-proportioned VW combi anybody has ever made" is an unverified superlative about an entire industry, in a document whose method forbids exactly that, and it is the one line that reads as pitch rather than object.
* WOULD FIX IT: FIVE CONCRETE EDITS. The first two decide dangerIntact on the next pass.

1. WELD THE TRAPDOOR SHUT. Delete "the apertures get a scale of their own and THAT number goes on the underside". Replace with: the holes are 25.775 x 20.15 mm, r 2.75, 512.9 mm2 — the measured apertures at 1:20 — and nothing else, ever. If it does not speak, the underside reads "512.9 mm2 — NO NOTE" and he is handed a bus that stays silent, which is a truer object about THIS vehicle than one that sings. Two outcomes, both shippable, neither negotiable. That restores the wager and costs nothing.

2. MOULD THE PROVENANCE OF THE SHAPE, NOT ONLY ITS ABSENCES. The underside currently confesses what is MISSING. Make it confess what is GUESSED. Five lines, every one already watched-printed in the repo:
       515.5 x 403.0 / 20
       OFF SIDE: NEVER PHOTOGRAPHED
       TAIL BOARD 38.0 deg -- A POSE, ONE AFTERNOON
       MOTOR: NINGUNO -- TRANSMISSION: SOLD
       EMBLEM OMITTED, NOT LICENSED BY VOLKSWAGEN
   This is the single highest-value change available. It turns the object from "the accurate one" into "the honest one", it is unarguable against the record, and a child holding a bus one of whose flanks nobody has ever seen — and whose propped board is frozen at one day's angle — is this entire graphic-design program compressed into 203 mm of polypropylene.

3. PUT THE HAND BACK. No ink, but mould the Senor Tacombi script and the aperture surrounds as RELIEF you can feel with a thumb. Near-zero tooling cost, restores the vehicle's identity, and re-opens SPEC 10.10 honestly instead of dodging it by blankness.

4. STOP APOLOGISING FOR THE NOISE. Rewrite risk (2) from "a dining room nobody wants to sit in" to the claim itself: this vehicle's noise was sold, and the whistle returns it to the room in the wrong voice from the wrong mouths. Still hand it at the door on the way out — as a decision, not a concession. He can still veto it; the concept must not veto itself first.

5. ADD THE MISSING SENTENCE, and do not resolve it: a working vehicle that fed people is being handed back to children as a souvenir with its engine gone. One sentence, left standing, no "heritage", no consolation.

CUT: "the first accurately-proportioned VW combi anybody has ever made." Unverifiable, forbidden by the project's own rule 5, and the only line in the piece that sounds like marketing.

DO NOT CUT, if a later pass gets nervous: the regulatory cost paragraph, the shared-mouthpiece line, the moulded VW disclaimer, and "IT MAY NOT SPEAK". Those four are the concept.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

### RUIDO — audit mean 1.62/4

**FEASIBILITY — 1.5/4 — NOT BUILDABLE AS WRITTEN, BUT THE FIX IS SMALL, CHEAP AND DOES NOT TOUCH THE IDEA. The concept is right that it is the only item in the pool that needs nothing from the machine — no line pass, no AO pass, no front-camera fix, no glTF export, no five-minute frames, no determinism problem. That is real and it is the strongest feasibility argument any concept in this programme has. But the plan mistakes "needs no Blender" for "needs nothing," and everything it actually needs is unpriced: a printmaker's skill nobody on this team has, source frames that cannot support portraiture, photographs nobody can prove they own, releases from identifiable people including a small child, a paper size that does not fit the press it names, and an ink that does not exist on that press. Two of the eight sheets are the same man. One prints the founder's own sentence under a stranger. One builds an action that appears in no photograph. Its single measured figure is in the wrong reference frame. And the proposed first step — hand the owner a drawing of a customer's toddler, made from an unattributed photograph, this week — is the most exposed object in the entire eight, chosen to go first.**

* ATTACKS: I LOOKED AT EVERY FRAME AND MEASURED EVERY FIGURE (rule 1, rule 8). Six of the seven people are real, which the last critic pass denied — CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md:157 calls "magenta puffer coat, back to camera" a fabrication because it is in no .md file. It is in the photograph. ref_nolita_front34.jpg has a blonde toddler in a magenta puffer at the bumper; ref_side.jpg has the man in the red shirt eating against the open cab door and the cook in the white cap; ref_rear34.jpg has a woman at a table with her chin on her hand; ref_workshop.jpg has a woman standing in the freshly cut roof of the green bus. That critic reasoned about the record; this concept looked. Credit where due. Now the feasibility.

(1) THE SOURCE CANNOT CARRY PORTRAITURE, AND THE CEILING IS UNSTATED. Cropped and measured at native resolution: EL DE LA CAMISA ROJA 140 x 370 px, face ~40 px. EL COCINERO 100 x 170 px, face ~35 px in profile. LA NINA 100 x 177 px. LA MUJER DE LA MESA 150 x 340 px, face ~60 px. LA QUE LO ABRIO 150 x 160 px, back of head only. EL MESERO 110 x 272 px. Two of the eight sheets come off ref_side.jpg, which SURVEY_rev49_photoreal.md:965 records as "the worst frame this project owns — 2.32 bits/px at JPEG DC quantiser 4." You cannot trace these; there is nothing to trace. The drawing must be INVENTED around a blur, which lands in the worst of both places — the likeness is not reliable AND it is still recognisable. That is not "one afternoon."

(2) SHEETS 2 AND 7 ARE THE SAME MAN. EL COCINERO's caption ("37 % of the VARIANCE is the man") and EL VENDEDOR's ("a pale band turned out to be the vendor's shirt") both trace to ref_side.jpg. SURVEY_rev49_photoreal.md:965 names its occluders: "a vendor standing in the roof opening, a man leaning on the flank and a palm crossing the board" — two people, and HANDOFF_rev12.md:172's "~37 % of that variance is the man working INSIDE" is the same vendor, seen through bay 1. I cropped it: one man in a white cap, his body visible through the bay-1 glass below his head. The set claims seven human beings; the record supports six. Two sheets of one man captioned as two people is a content defect print quality cannot fix, and the owner — who employed him — sees it first.

(3) THE CONSENT ANALYSIS NAMES THE WRONG SHEET, AND ITS MITIGATION IS FALSE ON INSPECTION. The concept says sheet 3 is safest because the child is "from behind exactly as the photograph has her, so no face exists to reproduce." Enlarged 4x: her head is TURNED. Cheek, brow line and ear are in frame. Meanwhile sheet 4, treated as ordinary, is a young woman FRONT-ON, face fully legible at ~60 px, bare-shouldered in a halter top, photographed candidly at a table, to be captioned "UNMEASURED," printed and sold. That is the letter. WORKFLOW_rev76_SYNTHESIS.md:476 already identified this exact person in another concept — "Panel 1 carries ONE fully legible front-on face (the seated woman)" — and recommended cropping her out. This concept does the opposite and does not mention that she is the front-on one.

(4) NOBODY OWNS THE PHOTOGRAPHS AND IN 76 REVISIONS NOBODY HAS ASKED. I grepped the whole tree for provenance, photographer, licence or source URL on any ref_* frame: nothing. Not one. The sizes are review-site and blog thumbnails — 700x467, 550x412, 450x450, 480x320. A hand-drawn derivative of a photograph is a derivative work of that photograph, and the photographer's copyright is entirely separate from the subject's likeness. The concept's risk paragraph — which is otherwise the most honest in the pool — does not mention copyright once. This is a GATE, not a risk line: if the answer to "who took ref_side.jpg" is "we found it," then sheets 1, 2, 4, 5 and 7 cannot be published as drawn.

(5) SHEET 5 PRINTS A MISATTRIBUTION, AND IT IS THE FOUNDER'S OWN SENTENCE. "I figured out how to cut open the Tacombi" is SPEC.md section 0.1, grade S, attributed verbatim to founder Dario Wolos. Sheet 5 titles the unidentified woman in ref_workshop.jpg "LA QUE LO ABRIO" — the woman who opened it — and puts his line under her. Same section also flags that the workshop bus is a livery/identity question, not established as this vehicle. This is rule 34 (a quote inherits its object) committed in ink, on a sheet handed to the man who knows whose sentence it is.

(6) SHEET 6's ACTION EXISTS IN NO PHOTOGRAPH. "Reaching for eight white plates": grep returns no such count anywhere. I cropped the only plate stack in the reference set — ref_nolita_flank.jpg, upper right — and it is roughly twenty-plus plates in two stacks sitting ON THE ROOF, with no person near them, in a different frame from EL MESERO (ref_nolita_front34b.jpg, where his hands are empty). The sheet fuses two frames and prints a count nobody watched print, in a project whose rule 5 is exactly that.

(7) THE ONE MODEL FIGURE IS IN THE WRONG FRAME. CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860 checks out — t1_detail.py:1613, a measured fraction over 113 columns, correctly stated as a fraction. But "the slab top at 1.2540 m above ground" is NOT above ground. t1_detail.py's own comment twenty lines above the constant says the built slab "reads 1.240 AG at its mean station against REF sec.6's 1.189 / 1.205," and warns in as many words not to convert it. The sheet's sole measured content is mislabelled by 14–65 mm, and CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md:119 already caught another concept making this identical error.

(8) THE FORMAT DOES NOT FIT THE PRESS, AND THE INK DOES NOT EXIST ON IT. 280 x 430 mm exceeds A3 (297 x 420) on the long edge, and A3 is the maximum drum on every standard Risograph duplicator — with a real print area nearer 291 x 413. As specified, none of the eight sheets can be risographed. Separately, sheet 8 is "gold on maroon": Riso has fluorescent pink (sheet 3 is fine) but there is no metallic gold drum. Gold means letterpress foil or metallic offset, a different process and a different price, on the one sheet that is supposed to be the emotional landing.

(9) THE COST LINE IS OUT BY TEN TO THIRTY TIMES, AND IT IS WRONG ON THE ONLY LINE THAT MATTERS. Print is genuinely cheap and the concept is roughly right there: riso, 8 designs x 200, one colour, coloured stock is about $600–1,200 with setup; letterpress from photopolymer, 8 plates x 200 at that size, is about $3,000–6,000. But the cost is not the printing. A printmaker who can work in the Posada/TGP register and build a whole standing figure from 100 x 170 px is 4–8 weeks and $8,000–25,000 for eight plates, not "a week of an illustrator's time." The AI cannot substitute: AI-generated portraits of identifiable real people from candid photographs is precisely what converts the consent risk from a quiet exposure into a public one. Honest total for the set: $12,000–35,000 and 8–12 weeks. Honest total for the single pilot sheet: $1,500–3,000 and 3–4 weeks, not an afternoon.

(10) THE FIRST STEP IS THE WORST AVAILABLE FIRST STEP. "Sheet 3, this week, put it in his hand and ask him nothing." The first physical object this programme delivers would be a hand-drawn picture of a stranger's small child, made from a photograph the company may not own, with no vehicle on it, from a 76-revision vehicle-modelling project, to an owner who has just said "we need to finally present something." Even if he loves it, his first sentence is "who is this child and do we have permission," and there is no answer in this repository.

WHAT SURVIVES ALL OF THAT: the reversal is sound, the register is genuinely new to the programme, the machine cost really is zero, and the captions are the record's own words rather than invented pathos. The idea is not the problem. The plan is.
* WOULD FIX IT: RE-SHOOT INSTEAD OF RE-DRAWING THE ARCHIVE. This is the whole fix and it is one afternoon plus a photographer's day rate. Six of these seven people, or their successors, stand at a Tacombi counter today. Commission one photographer for one service, get signed releases on the spot, and draw from 4000-px references. That collapses four separate blockers at once — the resolution ceiling, the photographers' copyright gap, the likeness exposure, and the "invented around a blur" likeness problem — and it costs about $1,500, less than the illustration of a single sheet. Only sheet 5 (green bus, workshop) genuinely needs the archive, and it needs a different fix anyway.

Then, in order:

1. CHANGE THE PILOT SHEET. Ship EL COCINERO first, not LA NINA. He is staff, he is photographable again today, he is in profile under a cap, he carries the strongest quote in the set, and he can sign a release. The whole rights problem goes from unanswerable to a form. Draw sheet 3 second, once the process is proven and after the owner has said yes to the register.

2. ASK ONE QUESTION BEFORE ANY DRAWING. Multiple choice, question tool, per the project's own method: "Who took ref_side.jpg, ref_rear34.jpg, ref_workshop.jpg and ref_nolita_front34.jpg, and do we hold the rights?" (a) Tacombi's own / we hold them; (b) a named photographer we can license from; (c) found online, provenance unknown; (d) don't know. If the answer is (c) or (d), five sheets are dead as drawn and the re-shoot becomes the only route — which is where you wanted to be anyway.

3. MERGE 2 AND 7 INTO ONE SHEET, EL DE LA GORRA BLANCA, carrying both quotes. Then find a real eighth: the second woman walking behind the sign in ref_rear34.jpg, or the man with the papers and apron at the left edge of ref_nolita_front34.jpg. Six people plus two constructed sheets, honestly labelled, beats a claimed seven.

4. SHEET 4: RELEASE OR EMPTY CHAIR. Either she is identified and signs, or the sheet becomes the chair, the table and the two glasses with nobody in it, captioned UNMEASURED. That is a better sheet — the record never touched her, and an empty chair says so more sharply than a portrait does — and it removes the single highest-exposure object in the programme.

5. FIX SHEET 5's CAPTION. Wolos's sentence goes back to Wolos or comes off the sheet. Either identify the woman and caption her with her own words, or retitle so the sheet does not claim she cut the roof. Ask the owner who she is — he probably knows, and if she is family the sheet becomes better, not worse.

6. DELETE OR GROUND "EIGHT WHITE PLATES." Either count them in a named frame and print the count with the frame, or drop the number. Same for the counter: print 1.240 m AG at the mean station, or print the disagreement (authored 1.2540 un-dropped / built 1.240 AG / photographed 1.189–1.205), which is more in the spirit of the set than any single figure. Keep CNT_NOSE_F 0.1860 exactly as written — it is correct and it is stated in the right units.

7. FIX THE FORMAT BEFORE THE FIRST DRAWING, NOT AT THE PRINTER. Either 280 x 400 mm, which fits an A3 Riso with bleed, or commit to letterpress at 280 x 430 and budget $3–6k. Decide sheet 8's gold now: Riso has no metallic, so it is foil, a metallic offset, or an ochre that is honest about not being gold.

8. PUBLISH THE BUDGET WITH THE PITCH, WITH ITS CEILING, the way this project publishes every other measurement. Pilot sheet: $1,500–3,000, 3–4 weeks. Full set: $12,000–35,000, 8–12 weeks, of which the print is under 10%. Say that the binding constraint is an illustrator this team does not have and that the AI cannot stand in for one on portraits of real people. A concept that prices itself honestly at thirty times its first estimate is still by far the cheapest new register in the programme — and it is the only one that ships while Blender is busy.

None of this changes the idea, the eight subjects, the captions, the one-ink economics or the reversal. It changes where the photographs come from and which sheet goes first.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

**ORIGINALITY — 1/4 — DERIVATIVE ON THREE INDEPENDENT AXES AND ALREADY THE FIFTH COPY INSIDE ITS OWN POOL. The register change is real; the idea underneath it is the bench's measurement-caption engine with a brush instead of a hairline, and its core move belongs to three canonical bodies of work it names none of. One sheet (5) and one instinct (commission the people) are salvageable; the set as specified is not fresh.**

* ATTACKS: **1 · IT IS AT LEAST THE FIFTH CONCEPT IN ITS OWN BENCH DOING THIS, AND TWO OF THEM QUOTE THE SAME SENTENCES.** Grepped, not asserted:

- `CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md:1508` — *"The six people this project spent seventy-six revisions deleting as measurement error, drawn back in by a human hand"* — then quotes the red shirt, the 37 % of variance, and the identical residuals −16.7 / −16.1 / −13.6 px.
- `:405` (people-1) — *"Cut the bus out of its own photographs, and what is left on the card is the people this project spent seventy-six revisions calling noise"*, with the deleting line of source code printed on the reverse.
- `:1186` (packaging-2) — a napkin carrying *"37 % of the variance is the man, and no dressing can produce it."*
- `:577` — the cook life-size, *"the record has spent forty revisions treating him as contamination — 'a pale band turned out to be the vendor's shirt'."*
- `:2183` — the same two quotes again as an ethical note.

RUIDO's sheets 1, 2, 3, 7 are people-1's four cards. Its sheet-2 caption is already a bench TITLE (**THIRTY-SEVEN PER CENT**). The bench's own critic has already bracketed `[dream:people-1]` and `[dream:free-8-3]` together twice — once as *"Smejkal's Fatescapes inverted"* (`:123`) and once on the art-school cull shelf (`:128`). A concept whose distinguishing claim is *"in a programme where eleven of eleven deleted the people, this one deletes the vehicle"* is false on its own bench: five concepts deleted the vehicle first.

**2 · THE CORE MOVE IS THREE FAMOUS BODIES OF WORK, NONE CITED.**

*(a) The incidental human recovered from a machine's capture* — Jon Rafman, **9-Eyes of Google Street View** (2008–); Michael Wolf, **A Series of Unfortunate Events** (2010, World Press Photo); Doug Rickard, **A New American Picture** (2010, MoMA). Rescuing the person who was accidental to an automated survey, and presenting them as a portrait, is a fifteen-year-old genre with a canon.

*(b) The machine's own dismissive label printed as the caption under the face* — Paglen & Crawford, **ImageNet Roulette / Training Humans** (Fondazione Prada, 2019) and *Excavating AI*; Joy Buolamwini, **AI, Ain't I A Woman?** (2018), which is literally portraits captioned with the system's contemptuous misclassification; Adam Harvey, **MegaPixels / Exposing.ai**; Mimi Onuoha, **The Library of Missing Datasets** (2016) — which is sheet 4's *"UNMEASURED"* as a complete artwork. This is not obscure prior art. Between 2018 and 2022 it was the single most exhibited gesture in critical-technology art. Sheets 1–7 are that gesture, hand-drawn.

*(c) The numbered one-block, one-colour print series of working people with declarative captions, on TGP economics* — **Elizabeth Catlett, *The Negro Woman*, Taller de Gráfica Popular, 1946–47**: fifteen linocuts, one block each, ordinary working women, one declarative caption per sheet, cheap, distributed. Also Carlos Cortez's IWW linocut broadsides, Artemio Rodríguez. RUIDO's guardrail — *"his economics, not one calavera"* — is aimed at a threat nobody faced. Posada was never the risk. **Catlett is**, and the concept does not see her, because it took the TGP's form believing the form was empty. It was not: the numbered series of one-ink worker portraits *is* the TGP's most reproduced format.

**3 · AS MERCHANDISE IT IS THE DEFAULT PITCH.** Hand-drawn portraits of the people around a food business, one colour, riso, coloured stock, given away — that is the first slide of every hospitality-branding deck since about 2013, and Tacombi already runs a Mexican-vernacular illustration house style. Strip the captions and RUIDO is a staff-portrait wall. The concept's own protection is the caption, which returns us to attack 2b. Riso-on-coloured-uncoated at 280 × 430 buys zero distinctiveness; it is the ambient default of every art-book fair for a decade.

**4 · IT IS THE MEASURED-LINE AESTHETIC UNDER A NEW LABEL — AND THIS IS THE strongest version of that charge in the pool.** The *register* changes: hand, ink, paper. The **rhetorical engine does not.** The payload of every sheet is a measurement quotation in small type: `37 % of the VARIANCE`, `residuals −16.7, −16.1, −13.6 px`, `UNMEASURED`. That is NO LEGIBLE's move (specimens plus the record's own failure) and THE UNCERTAINTY PLATE's move (unmeasured set as a confession) applied to faces. The bench critic's finding at `:88` names it: *"roughly forty of these objects print their own uncertainty on their own face… a company that apologises on every surface it owns, in 6 pt, to a customer holding lunch."* RUIDO is eight more, with a person above the apology. And finding `:128` supplies the kill test, which RUIDO fails: **the idea lives in a caption you can cut off**, and cutting it off leaves the generic object of attack 3.

**5 · THE ONE DETAIL THAT MAKES SHEET 3 DRAWABLE IS FABRICATED, AND THE BENCH ALREADY CAUGHT IT.** `CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md:159`: `grep -rin 'puffer|magenta' *.md` returns only stars and a mask colour. F220 holds **three pixel values on hair** — (205,165,157), (204,172,161), (193,166,149) — *no garment, no orientation*. So "magenta puffer coat, from behind" is invented, and it is simultaneously (i) the drawing's only descriptive content, (ii) the entire consent mitigation, and (iii) the colour of the ink. By this project's own rule — never publish a figure you have not watched print — the flagship sheet is unbuilt. An originality claim resting on a specific, particular child dissolves when the particulars are made up; what is left is a generic child, which is stock.

**6 · IT IS PORTABLE, WHICH IS THE TELL.** The model contributes one constant and the concept says so proudly. Swap this archive for any dataset with people in it and the eight sheets survive unchanged. Nothing in RUIDO requires *this* vehicle, *this* taqueria, or 76 revisions — only *a* record that was rude about *some* people. Originality of occasion is zero. Compare sheet 5, which is the exception and the way out.

**7 · WHAT IS GENUINELY NEW, STATED FAIRLY.** Three things survive. (i) Sheet 5, LA QUE LO ABRIÓ — a woman standing in a roof she has just cut open, captioned with *her own sentence*, on a green bus in 2006. No prior art touches that; it is unrepeatable and it belongs to this company alone. (ii) Sheet 8's structure — two hands and a plate crossing one measured gold rule — is the only place the machine and the hand meet on equal terms. (iii) The instinct that this programme has been cold and that the correction is people. That instinct is right. The execution is other people's.
* WOULD FIX IT: **A · COMMISSION THE PEOPLE. This is the single fix that defeats every prior art at once.** Every antecedent above — Rafman, Wolf, Rickard, Paglen, Buolamwini, Smejkal, and Catlett by analogy — is an artist acting *on* subjects taken from an archive. **Nobody has made the version where the business that took the photographs goes back, finds the seven people, pays them, and prints a sheet each carrying their own sentence and their own name in the colophon.** That is not appropriation and not a staff-portrait wall; it is a commission and a public correction, and it is the only version with no close precedent. It simultaneously dissolves the consent risk, the "designer sneering" risk the concept itself calls fatal, and the fabrication problem (you can ask her what colour the coat was). Budget: a fixer, a week, seven fees. Cheaper than the letter.

**B · CUT THE MACHINE-CAPTION DEVICE FROM SEVEN OF THE EIGHT SHEETS.** Print the record's language **once** — a colophon block on the wrapper or sheet 8, set as the indictment. On the sheets, print what a broadside actually carried: a **notice**. An hour, a price, a dish, a date, a closure. TGP economics means the TGP's *function* (one block, coloured stock, given away the day it happened), not its subject matter. This one edit removes the Paglen/Buolamwini collision, removes the bitterness, and stops the set being finding-88's forty-first disclaimer.

**C · USE THE ONE MEASUREMENT THAT WAS AFFECTIONATE, NOT THE CONTEMPTUOUS ONES.** `REF_MEASUREMENTS.md:78` measured the leaning man at **375 px = 1.78 m at 211 px/m**, correctly, *with a stated ceiling* — and then used him as a ruler check and threw him away. That is a human being measured properly and discarded, and it is unused by every other concept in the pool. Print **his sheet at his measured height**, and each other sheet at whatever the record honestly holds about that person. The ruler becomes recognition instead of contempt, the set stops being quotable-as-cruelty, and the numbers are real ones you can watch print.

**D · BREAK THE PORTRAIT GRID (this is the Catlett escape).** Eight numbered single-figure worker portraits in one ink is her format. Make **six of the eight non-portraits**: hands, a queue, a back at a counter, a cap on a hook, two plates crossing. Keep one whole figure. Lower identifiability, off both Catlett's and the staff-wall's turf, and it makes LA ENTREGA the *form* of the set rather than its punchline.

**E · CHANGE THE FIRST STEP FROM SHEET 3 TO SHEET 5.** Sheet 3 has a minor, no consent path, and invented content. **Sheet 5 has a real quoted sentence, a findable named person who can consent and be credited, and a subject that exists in no other archive on earth.** It is the least derivative sheet and the only one that can actually ship this week. Test the register where it can survive contact.

**F · EITHER OPEN `ref_nolita_front34.jpg` AND PAINT THE WINDOW, OR DELETE THE PUFFER.** The record holds three hair pixels. Draw only that, or go and look. Do not let a fabricated garment carry the consent argument.

**G · NAME THE LINEAGE ON THE COLOPHON** — Smejkal, Rafman, Paglen & Crawford, Catlett/TGP. The bench's own critic found that the two concepts naming their precedents are the two strongest in the pool (`:69`). A precedent named is a position; a precedent unnamed is a discovery waiting for a journalist.

**H · REPLACE THE GUARDRAIL, WHICH IS AIMED AT THE WRONG TARGET.** Not "no calaveras" — nobody was drawing one. The real rules: **no numbered single-figure worker portrait series in one ink** (Catlett); **no machine label set under a face** (Paglen, Buolamwini); **no found-incidental-capture framing** (Rafman, Wolf); **no staff-portrait wall** (every restaurant since 2013).

**I · DE-DUPLICATE BEFORE ANY OF THIS.** One owner for the people. Merge people-1, free-2-1, packaging-2, photographic-1 and RUIDO into a single object and let RUIDO own it **only if** it takes fix A — because commissioning is the one thing none of the other four proposes, and it is the only claim to freshness the concept can still make.

**TEST TO RUN BEFORE RESUBMITTING:** delete every occurrence of "combi", "Tacombi" and "the bus" from the eight captions. If the set still reads intact, it is not about this vehicle and any other archive can make it. It currently passes that test with room to spare — that is the diagnosis, and fixes A, C and E are the only ones that make it fail.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

**OWNER-AND-BRAND — 1/4 — REJECT AS SPECIFIED — salvageable kernel, unshippable artefact. The premise (the record deleted the people; put them back) is the strongest emotional move in the bench and F331's own text keeps the PEOPLE territory open. But as drawn, RUIDO is eight sheets a restaurateur cannot hand to a guest: it carries no brand mark, no bus, and almost no food; it makes commercial use of identifiable non-consenting customers and a minor in New York; it captions his own staff in the record's engineering contempt; and its FIRST STEP explicitly instructs us to spend his money and his customers' likenesses without asking him. He would not be proud to hand someone this. He would ask where the bus is.**

* ATTACKS: 1. THE FIRST STEP IS THE WORST LINE IN THE CONCEPT, AND IT IS AN OWNER-FIT DEFECT, NOT AN ETHICS ONE. "Put it in his hand and ask him nothing." The single highest-consent-risk item in a 75-concept bench is the one that proposes to skip the ruling. Every other item in this programme goes to him as multiple choice with the reference attached (F330 and F331 were both got that way, and both changed the programme). Worse is the stated motive: "he has been told for seventy-six revisions that the child in his photograph was noise, and this is the first object that disagrees." That is an artefact built to make a point to the owner ABOUT OUR OWN RECORD. He has never read probe_rev39_flank.py. The emotional payload is a methodological apology from us to us, and he is being cast as the person who needs to receive it. Category error about who the audience is.

2. LIKENESS. NEW YORK. A MINOR. COMMERCIAL USE. The concept states this risk and then understates it. NY Civil Rights Law §50/51 covers a "portrait or picture" used for advertising or trade without written consent; recognizable drawings are covered, and a minor's consent must be a parent's, in writing. "Given away or sold for a few dollars in the room" is trade use — free does not help, it is promotional. And the mitigations run backwards: "identification carried by posture and clothing rather than face" preserves recognizability to exactly the people who would complain (her parents, his coworkers) while removing nothing legally. Sheet 1 is a CUSTOMER, not staff — REF_MEASUREMENTS.md:78 has him at 375 px = 1.78 m, a man eating at the counter — so there is no employment relationship to hang a release on. For a family taqueria the letter is not even the main damage; the story is ("restaurant sold drawings of a child who ate there, labelled with error figures").

3. THE CAPTIONS CANNOT BE RESCUED BY THE SEQUENCE, BECAUSE THE DISTRIBUTION DESTROYS THE SEQUENCE. The concept names its own killer — "'37 % of the variance is the man' said of your own cook can read as contempt" — and then names a mitigation that the artefact's own economics defeat: "the set works only if the sequence is affectionate and sheet 8 lands." A broadside handed to one person in a dining room is seen ALONE. Nobody receives eight sheets in order. Sheet 2 will be read on its own, by the cook, in the room where he works, and it says he is 37 % of the variance. And the FIRST STEP ships sheet 3 — the child, the most exposed of the eight — alone, first, with no sheet 8 anywhere near it. The tone-correcting device is structurally unavailable at the moment of delivery.

4. IT DELETES THE THING HE PAID FOR, AND THE FIG LEAF IS ONE FLOAT. "The bus appears on none of them." "MODEL: NOTHING." The contribution is CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860 as a rule on one sheet. He has 76 revisions, 229 mesh objects and 366,490 faces, and his standing complaints are "Why do we keep doing Revs that don't ship geometry" and "we need to finally present something." F331 sanctions PEOPLE as a register — it does not sanction deleting the vehicle from a vehicle programme. The bench's own best PEOPLE occupant (GRACIAS POR SU PREFERENCIA, 7.33) manages people WITH the brand present. This is the only concept where a guest who asks "what is this?" cannot be answered without narrating our engineering record.

5. SPEC §10.10 BITES ON EXACTLY ONE SHEET, AND IT IS THE ONE ELEMENT HE HAS ALREADY REJECTED BY NAME. Sheet 2's apron "carrying the Señor Tacombi script." §10.10 is a hard bar: painted elements must be REPRODUCED from photographs, read at 4–5× and redrawn, "not approximated... not a system font with flourishes bolted on — that is what shipped in rev 8 and Donald rejected it by name." An illustrator's freehand impression of that script, on a sheet given to customers, is precisely the failure mode the rule exists to stop, on the precise element that produced the rejection. Nothing else in the eight sheets touches §10.10 at all, so this is one line of fix, not a structural problem — but as written it is a live breach on a public-facing object.

6. WHERE IT IS ACTUALLY STRONG, AND I WILL NOT PRETEND OTHERWISE. VW TRADEMARK EXPOSURE IS THE LOWEST IN THE ENTIRE PROGRAMME — no roundel, no nose, no bus, so the nine emblem rejections and the trademark question are both moot here; this is the one concept where the emblem's brokenness costs nothing. The cost is real and small, it takes zero Blender time and does not compete for the four cores, and the register is genuinely absent from the bench. Sheet 5 (LA QUE LO ABRIÓ, the roof being cut open, the bus still green) is a brand-owned founding story the company already tells in its own words — that sheet he would frame.

7. THE F331 ARGUMENT EATS ITSELF. The concept labels the set ADULT LINE, then justifies sheet 3 on the grounds that "his only stated audience is children at the restaurant." If the child is the argument, this is not the children's-line object — and F331 explicitly says a children's line "of equal standing is owed" and does not yet exist. Using the children's brief to license an adult-line broadside about a child spends the strongest card in the file on the wrong table.
* WOULD FIX IT: CONCRETELY, IN ORDER — each of these is cheap and none of them removes the danger the concept is for:

A. DELETE "ask him nothing" AND REPLACE THE FIRST STEP WITH SHEET 5. Lead with LA QUE LO ABRIÓ — the woman in the freshly cut roof, bus still green, caption "I figured out how to cut open the Tacombi" (SPEC §0.1, graded S, the brand's own published account). Zero minor exposure, a subject the brand already puts its name to, the founding act rather than a measurement error, and it is the one sheet he would hang. Then ask him — question tool, multiple choice, that sheet attached, three options: (i) the full seven with releases, (ii) archetypes with no identifiable individual, (iii) this sheet alone as a one-off. Costs an afternoon and converts the set's biggest liability into his decision.

B. SPLIT THE SET BY CONSENT, DO NOT MITIGATE BY DRAWING STYLE. Staff (sheets 2, 6, 7) are employees of an operating restaurant — a written release for a portrait is one conversation and a credit line, and it turns the sheet from extraction into a gift. Sheet 5 is founder/brand material. Customers (sheets 1, 4) and the child (sheet 3) get no release realistically: redraw them as ARCHETYPES — different clothing, different bay, different posture, so the record's sentence survives and the identifiable individual does not. That kills the §50/51 exposure entirely and costs perhaps 20 % of the power. LA NIÑA is either cut or generic unless a parent signs; the concept's own "from behind, so no face exists" is not a defence and should stop being offered as one.

C. TWO-LINE CAPTIONS: THE CORRECTION, NOT THE INSULT. Every sheet gets the record's sentence in small type AND, larger, either the person's own name (with permission) or the correction. Not "37 % of the VARIANCE is the man" standing alone — "37 % of the variance is the man and the surfaces he occludes, AND NO DRESSING CAN PRODUCE IT" is already the record admitting he is irreplaceable; set the second clause large and the first small and the same string flips from contempt to tribute without inventing a word. That is the single highest-leverage edit in the whole concept and it changes no artwork.

D. PUT THE VEHICLE BACK ON EXACTLY ONE SHEET, AS A NINTH. The side orthographic camera fits and works TODAY (front and rear clip; do not use them). One 1:10 counter elevation, one ink, with the seven people's standing positions marked as ticks along it and the eight sheet titles set against them — CNT_ZT 1.2540 slab top, CNT_X0 0.9180 to CNT_X1 −2.1880, 3106.0 mm of counter. It costs one line-pass render, it makes the set legibly HIS rather than ours, and it gives the guest who asks "what is this?" a one-second answer.

E. REMOVE THE SCRIPT FROM THE APRON, OR REPRODUCE IT UNDER §10.10. Either draw the apron plain, or take the letterforms from script_gen.py's redrawn 4–5× replication and set them — never an illustrator's freehand impression of the one artwork element he rejected by name in rev 8.

F. STATE THE F331 LINE HONESTLY AND OWE THE OTHER ONE. This is the adult line; say so and stop citing the children's brief as its warrant. If the child is genuinely the reason this concept exists, the deliverable it argues for is a CHILDREN'S-line object — and F331 says one is owed and none exists. Propose that separately, where the argument actually lands.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

**DANGER — 3/4 — DANGER LARGELY INTACT — this is one of maybe three concepts in the whole programme with something actually at stake, and it did NOT get sanded into a warning label. But three specific edges have been traded away or left unowned, and one of them is a live wire the concept does not know it is holding.

WHAT IS GENUINELY DANGEROUS HERE, NAMED PROPERLY:
(1) It is an accusation against the client's own paid work, printed as an object and handed out in his own room. Seven sheets say: you paid for seventy-six revisions of a machine that classified your customers and your cook as error. That is the real risk, larger than consent, and the concept states it second ("the captions are bitter") when it is first.
(2) A minor's likeness, drawn and sold, commercially, in New York.
(3) The captions are the record's own contempt, quoted verbatim, under drawings of people who may still work there. "37 % of the VARIANCE is the man" under a cook who could walk past the rack is the most uncomfortable single object this programme has produced.
(4) Sheet 4's caption is one word — "UNMEASURED." — under a woman waiting for food. That is the best thing in the set and it costs nothing to keep.

IT STILL CARRIES ALL FOUR. The mitigations offered are real and partial and are named as partial; blurring is explicitly refused; the risk section does not resolve itself. That is the correct posture and it is rare in this pool. dangerIntact = true.

WHERE IT HAS ALREADY BEEN SANDED, AND IT MATTERS:
(a) NOBODY LOOKS AT YOU. The sibling this descends from (dream:people-3, EL RUIDO ERA GENTE, CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md line ~409) has "a man eating, looking straight down the lens ... recognisable to anyone who knows him." RUIDO's sheet 1 is "leaning on the open cab door, eating" — the gaze is deleted. Direct address is the single mechanism that makes a viewer complicit rather than sympathetic. Trading photograph for drawing was right; trading the gaze away with it was not, and no risk paragraph asks for it back.
(b) THE SET ABSOLVES ITSELF. Eight sheets of indictment ending on LA ENTREGA — two hands, a plate, gold on maroon, the model's one contribution, the warm one — is a set that lets the buyer off at the door. The concept openly says the set "works only if the sequence is affectionate and sheet 8 lands." That is a designer pre-negotiating his own forgiveness. If sheet 8 is the last thing you see, sheet 3 costs the viewer nothing.
(c) TWO SHEETS ARE PADDING AND ONE BREAKS THE SET'S OWN RULE. The premise is "caption them in the record's own words." EL MESERO (6) has NO record caption — "reaching for eight white plates" is description, not quotation. EL VENDEDOR (7) duplicates EL COCINERO (2): both are the same class of occlusion complaint, and "a pale band turned out to be the vendor's shirt" is from SURVEY_rev49_photoreal.md's ceiling paragraph about the same man in the roof as sheet 5. Eight is a number reached by padding to eight. Six sheets each with a real quoted sentence is harder and better.**

* ATTACKS: ATTACK 1 — THE SAFETY ARGUMENT RESTS ON AN UNPAINTED WINDOW, WHICH IS THIS PROJECT'S OLDEST DEFECT (rule 8). "sheet 3 from behind exactly as the photograph has her, so no face exists to reproduce" is a claim in prose. Nothing in this repository records her pose. The bench's own critic already caught this class: CONCEPT_BENCH_rev77.md line 119 — "the people in seven concepts — the magenta puffer, the back to camera, the gaze into the lens — are in no file in this repository, and those unpainted windows are carrying the consent arguments." Worse, the record may hold TWO different children and this concept has merged them: F220 (OPEN_FINDINGS.md:433) puts a child AT THE BUMPER with residuals -16.7/-16.1/-13.6 px and pixel values (205,165,157) etc — flesh and hair, on a traced sagitta; LEDGER_rev47.md:263 and NEXT_CONTEXT_PROMPT_rev48.md:139 record a DIFFERENT fact, "a child's head occludes the lower quarter" of ref_nolita_front34.jpg. If sheet 3 is drawn from the nolita frame she may be facing the camera and the entire mitigation is void; if she is F220's child the drawing is being made from three residual points. The one sheet the concept calls "THE SHEET" is the one whose window was never opened.

ATTACK 2 — SHEET 5 IS A LIVE WIRE AND THE CONCEPT DOES NOT MENTION IT. "I figured out how to cut open the Tacombi" is DARIO WOLOS'S OWN SENTENCE, in the first person — SPEC.md:41-43, "Founder Dario Wolos ... he 'figured out how to cut open the Tacombi'." RUIDO prints that first-person founding claim under a drawing of a WOMAN and titles her LA QUE LO ABRIÓ, "the one who opened it." Read one way that is the most political object in the programme: an uncredited woman given the founder's own founding act, on merchandise sold in his restaurant. Read the other way it is a misattribution that will be read as either sloppiness or a slur. Both readings are available and the concept has chosen neither. Danger you have not decided to carry is danger that gets removed by the first person who notices it, at the worst possible moment, taking the rest of the set with it.

ATTACK 3 — SHEET 5 MAY NOT BE THIS BUS. "the bus still green" asserts ref_workshop.jpg is the target vehicle mid-conversion. The record is split against itself: HANDOFF_CARRIERS.md §0.1 lists ref_workshop.jpg under "NOT the target, geometry only — the GREEN vehicle", while SPEC.md:395 notes it "carries the same 'Señor Tacombi' script on the green body" and SPEC.md:10166 calls it "mid-conversion". The sibling concept states it outright as UNRESOLVED and says SPEC §7.2 forbids deciding it on livery colour. If it is not this vehicle, sheet 5 is a portrait of a stranger in an unrelated workshop sold as this company's origin story — a bigger consent problem than the child, and a factual one.

ATTACK 4 — THE STATED RISK IS RANKED WRONG, WHICH IS ITSELF A SANDING. "THESE ARE REAL, IDENTIFIABLE PEOPLE ... WHO DID NOT CONSENT" is listed first because it is the legible, lawyerable risk. The unlegible one — that this is an object criticising the client's own commissioned work, in his own room, in his own record's words — is listed second and softened to a tone problem ("if the tone slips by ten degrees"). It is not a tone problem. It is the content. A version that solved consent perfectly (every release signed) would still be the same object saying the same thing. Putting consent first is how this concept will get "fixed" into safety: someone will solve the releases, declare the risk retired, and ship it with the captions softened.

ATTACK 5 — F331 COMPLIANCE IS ASSERTED, NOT EARNED, AND IT IS DOING WORK. "ADULT LINE (F331)" is stamped at the top. F331 (OPEN_FINDINGS.md:559) does require every item to say its line, so the stamp is correct procedure — but it is also a shield: sheet 3 is explicitly justified by "his only stated audience is children at the restaurant, there is one in his own photograph," which is a CHILDREN'S-LINE argument used to license an ADULT-LINE object. You cannot claim the child sheet's moral force from F18's "spark joy ... for children" and then put it in the line children do not get. Pick one. The honest answer is that sheet 3 belongs in neither line and that is precisely why it is worth making.

ATTACK 6 — MODEL: NOTHING, AND THE ONE CONSTANT IS DECORATIVE. CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860 as a single gold rule on sheet 8 is a fig leaf; it is one number from t1_detail and nobody looking at a gold rule can see it. I do not hold this against the concept's danger — deleting the vehicle is a real refusal and it is stated as the concept — but be honest that it means the set has no defence except "it was cheap" if the owner reacts badly to being told, on paper, that his machine deleted his customers. Note also that the counter figure "1.2540 m above ground" is the AUTHORED UN-DROPPED constant the source itself warns about (STATE.md: geometry is authored un-dropped, RIDE_DROP subtracted last), so even the one figure is frame-confused. Do not print it.
* WOULD FIX IT: 1. PAINT THE WINDOW BEFORE YOU DRAW THE SHEET. Open the frames, crop each of the seven people, save the crops to probe_scratch/, and LOOK. Establish, per person and in writing: which frame, which pixels, facing where, face visible yes/no, resolution across the head in px. The consent argument and sheet 3's whole mitigation are currently prose. Until that crop exists, sheet 3 is not a design decision, it is a hope. This is one afternoon and it is the precondition for everything else.

2. DECIDE SHEET 5 EXPLICITLY, IN WRITING, ONE OF THREE WAYS. (a) Keep the founder's first-person sentence under her and OWN it — retitle so the transfer is deliberate and legible, and state in the colophon that the sentence is Dario Wolos's and that the record does not name her. That is the dangerous version and it is defensible. (b) Caption her with a sentence about her that IS in the record (the vendor/shirt/occlusion material) and drop the founding quote. (c) Cut the sheet. What you may not do is print a founder's "I" under an unnamed woman by accident. And resolve the green-bus identity first: if ref_workshop.jpg is not established as this vehicle, sheet 5 cannot be in this set at all.

3. PUT THE GAZE BACK ON ONE SHEET. Exactly one person must look out of the paper at whoever is holding it. Sheet 1 is the candidate — the record's own frame has him addressing the lens. Drawn, faceless-by-posture people are safe in the way that makes work die. One direct address, and the set stops being about them and starts being about the viewer.

4. RE-ORDER, AND DO NOT END WARM. LA ENTREGA is the correct sheet and the wrong ending. Make the last sheet LA MUJER DE LA MESA, caption "UNMEASURED." Put the hand-off at 7, or make it the wrapper/colophon rather than the finale. If the set must resolve, let it resolve one sheet before the end so the last thing in the hand is the void.

5. SIX SHEETS, NOT EIGHT — EVERY ONE WITH A QUOTED SENTENCE THAT EXISTS. Cut EL MESERO (no record caption) and fold EL VENDEDOR into EL COCINERO (same man, same complaint). Enforce the set's own rule: every caption is a verbatim string with a filename and line, greppable, printed in 6 pt on the reverse. That rule is what makes it an archive handed back rather than an illustrator's opinion, and it is currently broken on two of eight.

6. STATE THE REAL RISK FIRST, IN THE COLOPHON, IN ONE SENTENCE, SIGNED. Something to the effect of: this company paid for a machine that measured its bus and, seventy-six times, recorded the people at it as error; these are the people, in the machine's own words. If that sentence cannot be printed, the set should not be made. Putting it on the object is what stops the piece being sneered at as insolence — it makes the author, not the cook, the one who is exposed.

7. THE FIRST STEP IS RIGHT BUT INCOMPLETE. Sheet 3 alone, magenta on grey, is the correct first object. Add one thing: print the F220 residuals and the filename on the reverse, and put it in his hand with NOTHING said, as proposed. But do not print or sell ANY sheet until step 1's crops exist and step 2's decision is written down.
* **DANGER INTACT: True**

### PROVECHO — audit mean 1.50/4

**FEASIBILITY — 2/4 — FEASIBILITY VERDICT — PROVECHO: buildable, but costed backwards. The cheap half is priced to the dollar and the expensive half is a person the team does not have.

WHAT I VERIFIED IN THE SOURCE (so the concept is not resting on invented numbers — this is its strongest feasibility property and it is real):
- `t1_detail.py`: `CNT_ZT, CNT_ZB = 1.2540, 1.1470   # 107 mm thick` ✓; `CNT_Y_IN, CNT_Y_OUT = 0.8450, 1.1660   # 321 mm plan depth` ✓; `CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860` with the comment `# 19.9 mm of gold in elevation` ✓; `FRINGE_R, FRINGE_PITCH, FRINGE_INSET = 0.0090, 0.0260, 0.0130` ✓; `GAL_SEAM_X = -0.6140   # measured back-wall material change` ✓.
- `verify.py`: `BAY_CX_SPEC = (0.6720, 0.0470, -0.5980)` ✓.
- `t1_mats.py`: `M["brass"] = tarnished("brass", (0.6600, 0.4750, 0.1750), ...)` ✓.
- No human geometry and no food geometry anywhere — confirmed. The plancha is `T.rrect(0.560, 0.780, 0.02, seg=3)` extruded 0.530, i.e. a bare prism with nothing on it. The concept's self-declared unmeasured list is accurate.
- The "no camera stands at the pass" claim is TRUE and checkable: `studio.py`'s eye-height camera is `"playa": dict(loc=(3.15, 5.75, 1.60), ...)` — 5.75 m outboard, standing well back, not at a counter whose outer face is y 1.166.

THE FEASIBILITY LEDGER, HONESTLY:

CHEAP AND REAL (this concept is the least pipeline-dependent thing on the bench):
It needs no line pass, no render queue, no 3D export, no photoreal parity, and it does not care that the front/rear ortho cameras clip. The model contribution is a new entry in `studio.py`'s camera dict plus a Workbench/wireframe pass — seconds per frame, not the 5-minute Cycles path, so no tree freeze, no non-determinism, no `T1_PFX` bookkeeping. Half a day of machine work, and rev 77 already proved the design side can ship finished files (`design_out/`). The $35 dye-sub proof on 1 mm aluminium at 3–5 days is a real route at a real price.

SEVERE — THE ILLUSTRATOR IS AN UNSOURCED RESOURCE, AND HE IS THE ARTEFACT:
"2–3 days of one illustrator working over a model-derived perspective grid" is a line item for a person who does not exist on a team of one plus an AI. Everything else in the programme is DRAFTING — measured vector line, type, a rule-and-compass discipline the pipeline demonstrably has. This is PAINTING, at 1965-tin-tray quality, with two pairs of interacting hands and food. That is the one capability nobody here has demonstrated. Three routes and none is free: (a) commission — US$1,200–4,000 and 2–4 weeks of CALENDAR, not 2–3 days of labour; the concept conflates working days with schedule, and then invites a restaurateur to correct the food, which is two more rounds; (b) the owner paints it — unknown, and he is the impatient party; (c) generative + overpaint — ~US$0 and two days, but interacting hands holding a plate are the single worst-known failure mode, style consistency across the tray and the charolita crop is a re-roll lottery, and it will not respect the measured grid without heavy hand rework. The failure mode is not "it doesn't build" — it is "it builds and looks like clip art", and unlike a lotería card, whose line can be judged, this can only be judged by whether it is GOOD. There is no gate for that before the owner sees it.

TWO CONCRETE COSTING ERRORS IN THE TIN:
(1) "The charolita shares the plates" is wrong. A 240 × 180 tray is a different image size and a different pressed part — different plates (at best a ganged sheet, which then changes the trim and die layout) and certainly a second press tool. This roughly doubles the tooling line the concept quotes.
(2) The tooling is missing entirely. US$2,800 on six plates covers the LITHO. A 380 × 290 rolled-edge tray is a PRESSED part needing a blank/draw die and a curling tool — commonly US$1,500–5,000 — unless you print onto an existing stock tray footprint, in which case 380 × 290 is not yours to choose.
And "about 500 minimum" is unverified; tin litho tray MOQs commonly run 1,000–3,000. Honest first-pallet figure: US$6,000–12,000 and a pallet in a storeroom — not the US$2,800 + $2.20 the concept implies.

MODEL CONTRIBUTION IS OVERSTATED, AND THE CONCEPT ALMOST SAYS SO ITSELF:
The two things it claims the model uniquely gives — hand size and fringe density — follow from aperture size and counter height, which are two numbers an illustrator can use with a tape measure and no Blender. What the model uniquely gives is the correct APERTURE-PLANE perspective. Everything behind that plane — the galley recession the cook stands in, which is most of the picture — is invented, because Y is chosen everywhere in this model and never measured. The concept states this risk; it should also state the consequence, which is that the armature is worth half a day, not a headline.

LIKENESS: the cheap branch exists and the concept picks the expensive one. The owner employs the cook, so consent is obtainable — but it adds a person-in-the-loop, a release form, and a re-do if that employee leaves or declines. For v1 that is a dependency bought for nothing.

NOTHING HERE NEEDS A PHOTOGRAPHER, A BUS YOU CANNOT ACCESS, OR A CAPABILITY THAT DOES NOT EXIST IN THE WORLD. It needs a skill that does not exist ON THIS TEAM, and a manufacturing quote that has not been taken.**

* ATTACKS: 1. THE COST IS INVERTED. The tin is priced to the cent ($2,800 setup, $2.20 at 1,000, $1.60 at 5,000) and the PAINTING — which is the entire artefact; the tray is only a substrate — is "2–3 days of one illustrator". The precise number is on the disposable half.

2. THE ILLUSTRATOR DOES NOT EXIST. Team is one plus an AI. Every other concept on the bench is drafting, which this pipeline provably does (rev 77 shipped `line_pass.py` and two finished SVGs). This one is gouache painting at tray quality with two pairs of interacting hands and food. Commission = US$1,200–4,000 and 2–4 weeks calendar; generative = a hands lottery on the exact artefact whose whole argument is that it is beautiful.

3. "2–3 DAYS" IS LABOUR, NOT SCHEDULE. The concept's own first step invites the owner to correct the food. That is two review rounds. Realistic time to a thing in his hand: ~1 week self/generative, ~4 weeks commissioned. Not days.

4. "THE CHAROLITA SHARES THE PLATES" IS FALSE. Different image size, different pressed part — new plates and a second press tool. Concrete costing error.

5. THE PRESS TOOLING IS ABSENT FROM THE QUOTE. Six litho plates do not make a rolled-edge tray. A blank/draw die plus a curling tool is US$1,500–5,000, or you surrender the 380 × 290 dimension to a stock footprint.

6. MOQ 500 IS ASSUMED, NOT QUOTED. Tin tray litho commonly runs 1,000–3,000 minimum. First pallet is realistically US$6,000–12,000, not US$2,800 plus change.

7. THE MODEL'S CONTRIBUTION IS HALF A DAY, NOT A PILLAR. Verified: `studio.py`'s only eye-height camera is `"playa"` at loc (3.15, 5.75, 1.60) — 5.75 m back, not at a counter face at y 1.166 — so the concept is right that nothing stands at the pass, and right that a new camera fixes it. But that is a three-line dict entry plus a Workbench pass. Meanwhile the galley recession the cook occupies — most of the picture — is unmeasured Y, i.e. invented, so the armature is trustworthy only in the aperture plane.

8. NO GATE CAN CATCH THE FAILURE MODE. A lotería card fails visibly in the line; this fails as "generic taqueria illustration", which nothing in this repo can measure. The concept names this as a price paid on purpose (its risk 3) — correct — but it means the first honest quality check is the owner's face, after the money is spent.

9. THE LIKENESS BRANCH IS BOUGHT FOR NOTHING IN V1. "I would paint him and get the consent" adds a release form and a re-do risk to a first artefact that does not need it.

10. NOT AN ATTACK, RECORDED FOR FAIRNESS: every figure it cites verifies in the source — `CNT_ZT/ZB 1.2540/1.1470`, `CNT_Y_IN/OUT 0.8450/1.1660`, `CNT_NOSE_F = 0.1860` with "19.9 mm of gold in elevation" in the comment, `BAY_CX_SPEC (0.6720, 0.0470, -0.5980)`, `GAL_SEAM_X -0.6140`, `FRINGE_R/PITCH/INSET 0.0090/0.0260/0.0130`, brass linear (0.6600, 0.4750, 0.1750). And its unmeasured list is honest: there is genuinely no human and no food geometry, and the plancha is a bare `rrect(0.560, 0.780)` prism. That is why this scores 2 and not lower.
* WOULD FIX IT: RECLASSIFY THE DELIVERABLE. The artefact is A PAINTING. The tray is a substrate decision taken later. Do not quote tin, plates, MOQ or a charolita until the image exists and he has seen it. That alone removes every manufacturing error above from the critical path.

SHIP THE ONE PART THE MACHINE CAN ACTUALLY MAKE, THIS WEEK, AS A REAL ARTEFACT:
